===== M0002 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1740-1769
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub declarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1740 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1741 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1742 |         Node n = stub.node;
1743 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1744 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1745 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1746 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1747 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1748 | 
1749 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1750 |           continue;
1751 |         }
1752 | 
1753 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1754 |         // in the type registry.
1755 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1756 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1757 | 
1758 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1759 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1760 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1761 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1762 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1763 |               propName, unknownType, n);
1764 |         } else {
1765 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1766 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1767 |         }
1768 |       }
1769 |     }

===== M0005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 123-177
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
123 |   private void scanVars(Node n) {
124 |     switch (n.getType()) {
125 |       case Token.VAR:
126 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
127 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
128 |              child != null;) {
129 |           Node next = child.getNext();
130 |           declareVar(child);
131 |           child = next;
132 |         }
133 |         return;
134 | 
135 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
136 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 | 
140 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
141 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
142 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
143 |           return;
144 |         }
145 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
146 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
147 | 
148 |       case Token.CATCH:
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
151 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
152 |         // is the code block
153 | 
154 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
155 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
156 | 
157 |         declareVar(var);
158 |         scanVars(block);
159 |         return;  // only one child to scan
160 | 
161 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
162 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
163 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
164 |         break;
165 |     }
166 | 
167 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
168 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
169 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
170 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
171 |            child != null;) {
172 |         Node next = child.getNext();
173 |         scanVars(child);
174 |         child = next;
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken)

Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1757 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1758 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1759 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1760 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1761 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1762 |         next();
1763 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1764 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1765 |           next();
1766 |         }
1767 |         skipEOLs();
1768 |         token = next();
1769 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1770 |       }
1771 |     }
1772 |     return typeExpr;
1773 |   }

===== M0012 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 177-211
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
177 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
178 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
179 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
180 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
181 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
182 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
183 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
184 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
185 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
186 |             !thisParamType.checkEquivalenceHelper(
187 |                 otherParamType, eqMethod)) {
188 |           return false;
189 |         }
190 |       } else {
191 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
192 |           return false;
193 |         }
194 |       }
195 | 
196 |       // Check var_args/optionality
197 |       if (thisParam.isOptionalArg() != otherParam.isOptionalArg()) {
198 |         return false;
199 |       }
200 | 
201 |       if (thisParam.isVarArgs() != otherParam.isVarArgs()) {
202 |         return false;
203 |       }
204 | 
205 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
206 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
207 |     }
208 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
209 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
210 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
211 |   }

===== M0013 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1191-1199
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1191 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1192 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1193 |       return -1;
1194 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1195 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1196 |     } else {
1197 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1198 |     }
1199 |   }

===== M0015 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitParameterList(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1860-1894
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1860 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1861 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1862 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1863 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1864 | 
1865 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1866 |     int ordinal = 0;
1867 |     Node parameter = null;
1868 |     Node argument = null;
1869 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1870 |            (parameters.hasNext() ||
1871 |             parameter != null && parameter.isVarArgs())) {
1872 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1873 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1874 |       if (parameters.hasNext()) {
1875 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1876 |       }
1877 |       argument = arguments.next();
1878 |       ordinal++;
1879 | 
1880 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1881 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1882 |     }
1883 | 
1884 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1885 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1886 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1887 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1888 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1889 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1890 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1891 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1892 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1893 |     }
1894 |   }

===== M0016 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 458-483
Documentation:

Method code:
458 |   @Override
459 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
460 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
461 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
462 |     switch (n.getType()) {
463 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
464 |         // normal type checking
465 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
466 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
467 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
468 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
469 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
470 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
471 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
472 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
473 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
474 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
475 |         }
476 | 
477 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
478 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
479 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
480 |         break;
481 |     }
482 |     return true;
483 |   }

===== M0022 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 873-989
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the JsDoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
873 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
874 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
875 |         @Nullable String name,
876 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
877 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
878 | 
879 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
880 | 
881 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
882 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
883 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
884 |         if (var != null && var.getType() != null &&
885 |             var.getType().isFunctionType()) {
886 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = var.getType().toMaybeFunctionType();
887 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
888 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
889 |             functionType = aliasedType;
890 | 
891 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
892 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
893 |             }
894 |           }
895 |         }
896 |       }
897 | 
898 |       if (functionType == null) {
899 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
900 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
901 |             rValue != null && rValue.isFunction();
902 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
903 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
904 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
905 | 
906 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
907 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
908 | 
909 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
910 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
911 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
912 |             functionType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
913 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
914 |           }
915 |         }
916 | 
917 |         if (functionType == null) {
918 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
919 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(lvalueNode);
920 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
921 |           Var ownerVar = null;
922 |           String propName = null;
923 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
924 |           if (ownerName != null) {
925 |             ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
926 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
927 |               ownerType = ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType());
928 |             }
929 |             if (name != null) {
930 |               propName = name.substring(ownerName.length() + 1);
931 |             }
932 |           }
933 | 
934 |           ObjectType prototypeOwner = getPrototypeOwnerType(ownerType);
935 |           TemplateTypeMap prototypeOwnerTypeMap = null;
936 |           if (prototypeOwner != null &&
937 |               prototypeOwner.getTypeOfThis() != null) {
938 |               prototypeOwnerTypeMap =
939 |                   prototypeOwner.getTypeOfThis().getTemplateTypeMap();
940 |           }
941 | 
942 |           FunctionType overriddenType = null;
943 |           if (ownerType != null && propName != null) {
944 |             overriddenType = findOverriddenFunction(
945 |                 ownerType, propName, prototypeOwnerTypeMap);
946 |           }
947 | 
948 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
949 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
950 |                   scope)
951 |               .setContents(getFunctionAnalysisResults(fnRoot))
952 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenType, parametersNode)
953 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info, prototypeOwner)
954 |               .inferReturnType(info)
955 |               .inferInheritance(info);
956 | 
957 |           // Infer the context type.
958 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
959 |           if (ownerType != null && ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType() &&
960 |               ownerType.getOwnerFunction().hasInstanceType()) {
961 |             builder.inferThisType(
962 |                 info, ownerType.getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType());
963 |             searchedForThisType = true;
964 |           } else if (ownerNode != null && ownerNode.isThis()) {
965 |             // If 'this' has a type, use that instead.
966 |             // This is a hack, necessary because CollectProperties (below)
967 |             // doesn't run with the scope that it's building,
968 |             // so scope.getTypeOfThis() will be wrong.
969 |             JSType injectedThisType = ownerNode.getJSType();
970 |             builder.inferThisType(
971 |                 info,
972 |                 injectedThisType == null ?
973 |                 scope.getTypeOfThis() : injectedThisType);
974 |             searchedForThisType = true;
975 |           }
976 | 
977 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
978 |             builder.inferThisType(info);
979 |           }
980 | 
981 |           functionType = builder
982 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
983 |               .buildAndRegister();
984 |         }
985 |       }
986 | 
987 |       // all done
988 |       return functionType;
989 |     }

===== M0028 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.getType(StaticScope<JSType>,String,String,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 962-988
Documentation:
/**
   * Looks up a type by name. To allow for forward references to types, an
   * unrecognized string has to be bound to a NamedType object that will be
   * resolved later.
   *
   * @param scope A scope for doing type name resolution.
   * @param jsTypeName The name string.
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file where this reference appears.
   * @param lineno The line number of the reference.
   * @return a NamedType if the string argument is not one of the known types,
   *     otherwise the corresponding JSType object.
   */
Method code:
962 |   public JSType getType(StaticScope<JSType> scope, String jsTypeName,
963 |       String sourceName, int lineno, int charno) {
964 |     // Resolve template type names
965 |     JSType type = null;
966 |     JSType thisType = null;
967 |     if (scope != null && scope.getTypeOfThis() != null) {
968 |       thisType = scope.getTypeOfThis().toObjectType();
969 |     }
970 |     if (thisType != null) {
971 |       type = thisType.getTemplateTypeMap().getTemplateTypeKeyByName(jsTypeName);
972 |       if (type != null) {
973 |         Preconditions.checkState(type.isTemplateType(), "expected:" + type);
974 |         return type;
975 |       }
976 |     }
977 | 
978 |     type = getType(jsTypeName);
979 |     if (type == null) {
980 |       // TODO(user): Each instance should support named type creation using
981 |       // interning.
982 |       NamedType namedType =
983 |           new NamedType(this, jsTypeName, sourceName, lineno, charno);
984 |       unresolvedNamedTypes.put(scope, namedType);
985 |       type = namedType;
986 |     }
987 |     return type;
988 |   }

===== M0040 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3095-3116
Documentation:
/** Find the l-value that the given r-value is being assigned to. */
Method code:
3095 |   static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {
3096 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
3097 |     boolean isFunctionDeclaration = isFunctionDeclaration(n);
3098 |     if (isFunctionDeclaration) {
3099 |       return n.getFirstChild();
3100 |     } else if (parent.isName()) {
3101 |       return parent;
3102 |     } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3103 |       return parent.getFirstChild();
3104 |     } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent)) {
3105 |       return parent;
3106 |     } else if (
3107 |         (parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3108 |         parent.isOr() ||
3109 |         parent.isAnd() ||
3110 |         (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3111 |       return getBestLValue(parent);
3112 |     } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3113 |       return getBestLValue(parent);
3114 |     }
3115 |     return null;
3116 |   }

Method code:
573 |     private void attachLiteralTypes(Node n) {
574 |       switch (n.getType()) {
575 |         case Token.NULL:
576 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NULL_TYPE));
577 |           break;
578 | 
579 |         case Token.VOID:
580 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
581 |           break;
582 | 
583 |         case Token.STRING:
584 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
585 |           break;
586 | 
587 |         case Token.NUMBER:
588 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
589 |           break;
590 | 
591 |         case Token.TRUE:
592 |         case Token.FALSE:
593 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
594 |           break;
595 | 
596 |         case Token.REGEXP:
597 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(REGEXP_TYPE));
598 |           break;
599 | 
600 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
601 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
602 |           if (info != null &&
603 |               info.getLendsName() != null) {
604 |             if (lentObjectLiterals == null) {
605 |               lentObjectLiterals = Lists.newArrayList();
606 |             }
607 |             lentObjectLiterals.add(n);
608 |           } else {
609 |             defineObjectLiteral(n);
610 |           }
611 |           break;
612 | 
613 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): If we ever support Array tuples,
614 |           // we will need to put ARRAYLIT here as well.
615 |       }
616 |     }

===== M0044 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 177-222
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
177 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
178 |     // TODO(user): regex literals as well.
179 |     switch (n.getType()) {
180 |       case Token.STRING:
181 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
182 |         return n.getString();
183 | 
184 |       case Token.NAME:
185 |         String name = n.getString();
186 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
187 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
188 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
189 |           return name;
190 |         }
191 |         break;
192 | 
193 |       case Token.NUMBER:
194 |         return getStringValue(n.getDouble());
195 | 
196 |       case Token.FALSE:
197 |         return "false";
198 | 
199 |       case Token.TRUE:
200 |         return "true";
201 | 
202 |       case Token.NULL:
203 |         return "null";
204 | 
205 |       case Token.VOID:
206 |         return "undefined";
207 | 
208 |       case Token.NOT:
209 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
210 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
211 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? "false" : "true"; // reversed.
212 |         }
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
216 |         return arrayToString(n);
217 | 
218 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
219 |         return "[object Object]";
220 |     }
221 |     return null;
222 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 473-543
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
473 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
474 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
475 |     if (argsParent == null) {
476 |       if (info == null) {
477 |         return this;
478 |       } else {
479 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // arguments
484 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
485 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
486 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
487 |     }
488 | 
489 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
490 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
491 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
492 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
493 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
494 |     boolean isVarArgs = false;
495 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
496 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
497 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
498 | 
499 |       // type from JSDocInfo
500 |       JSType parameterType = null;
501 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
502 |       isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
503 | 
504 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
505 |         parameterType =
506 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
507 |       } else if (arg.getJSDocInfo() != null && arg.getJSDocInfo().hasType()) {
508 |         parameterType =
509 |             arg.getJSDocInfo().getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
510 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
511 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
512 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
513 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
514 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
515 |       } else {
516 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
517 |       }
518 | 
519 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
520 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
521 |           isOptionalParam,
522 |           isVarArgs);
523 | 
524 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
525 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
526 |       }
527 |     }
528 | 
529 |     // Copy over any old parameters that aren't in the param list.
530 |     if (!isVarArgs) {
531 |       while (oldParameterType != null && !isVarArgs) {
532 |         builder.newParameterFromNode(oldParameterType);
533 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
534 |       }
535 |     }
536 | 
537 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
538 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, formatFnName());
539 |     }
540 | 
541 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
542 |     return this;
543 |   }

632 |   public Scope getScope() {
633 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
634 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
635 |       return scope;
636 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java

391 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
392 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
393 |       cursor++;
394 |       --ungetCursor;
395 |       if (charno == -1) {
396 |         charno = getOffset();
397 |       }

399 |     }
400 | 
401 |     for (;;) {
402 |       int c;
403 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
404 |         hitEOF = true;
405 |         if (charno == -1) {
406 |           charno = getOffset();
407 |         }

411 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
412 |

415 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {

417 |           c = '\n';
418 |         }
419 |       } else {

===== M0085 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.type.SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node,FlowScope,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/type/SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 123-263
Documentation:

Method code:
123 |   @Override
124 |   public FlowScope getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node condition,
125 |       FlowScope blindScope, boolean outcome) {
126 |     // Check for the typeof operator.
127 |     int operatorToken = condition.getType();
128 |     switch (operatorToken) {
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.NE:
131 |       case Token.SHEQ:
132 |       case Token.SHNE:
133 |       case Token.CASE:
134 |         Node left;
135 |         Node right;
136 |         if (operatorToken == Token.CASE) {
137 |           left = condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
138 |           right = condition.getFirstChild();
139 |         } else {
140 |           left = condition.getFirstChild();
141 |           right = condition.getLastChild();
142 |         }
143 | 
144 |         Node typeOfNode = null;
145 |         Node stringNode = null;
146 |         if (left.isTypeOf() && right.isString()) {
147 |           typeOfNode = left;
148 |           stringNode = right;
149 |         } else if (right.isTypeOf() &&
150 |                    left.isString()) {
151 |           typeOfNode = right;
152 |           stringNode = left;
153 |         }
154 |         if (typeOfNode != null && stringNode != null) {
155 |           Node operandNode = typeOfNode.getFirstChild();
156 |           JSType operandType = getTypeIfRefinable(operandNode, blindScope);
157 |           if (operandType != null) {
158 |             boolean resultEqualsValue = operatorToken == Token.EQ ||
159 |                 operatorToken == Token.SHEQ || operatorToken == Token.CASE;
160 |             if (!outcome) {
161 |               resultEqualsValue = !resultEqualsValue;
162 |             }
163 |             return caseTypeOf(operandNode, operandType, stringNode.getString(),
164 |                 resultEqualsValue, blindScope);
165 |           }
166 |         }
167 |     }
168 |     switch (operatorToken) {
169 |       case Token.AND:
170 |         if (outcome) {
171 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
172 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
173 |         } else {
174 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
175 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
176 |         }
177 | 
178 |       case Token.OR:
179 |         if (!outcome) {
180 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
181 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
182 |         } else {
183 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
184 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
185 |         }
186 | 
187 |       case Token.EQ:
188 |         if (outcome) {
189 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
190 |         } else {
191 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
192 |         }
193 | 
194 |       case Token.NE:
195 |         if (outcome) {
196 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
197 |         } else {
198 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |       case Token.SHEQ:
202 |         if (outcome) {
203 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
204 |         } else {
205 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
206 |         }
207 | 
208 |       case Token.SHNE:
209 |         if (outcome) {
210 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
211 |         } else {
212 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
213 |         }
214 | 
215 |       case Token.NAME:
216 |       case Token.GETPROP:
217 |         return caseNameOrGetProp(condition, blindScope, outcome);
218 | 
219 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
220 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
221 |             condition.getFirstChild(),
222 |             firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
223 |                 condition.getFirstChild().getNext(), blindScope, outcome),
224 |             outcome);
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NOT:
227 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
228 |             condition.getFirstChild(), blindScope, !outcome);
229 | 
230 |       case Token.LE:
231 |       case Token.LT:
232 |       case Token.GE:
233 |       case Token.GT:
234 |         if (outcome) {
235 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, ineq);
236 |         }
237 |         break;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
240 |         return caseInstanceOf(
241 |             condition.getFirstChild(), condition.getLastChild(), blindScope,
242 |             outcome);
243 | 
244 |       case Token.IN:
245 |         if (outcome && condition.getFirstChild().isString()) {
246 |           return caseIn(condition.getLastChild(),
247 |               condition.getFirstChild().getString(), blindScope);
248 |         }
249 |         break;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.CASE:
252 |         Node left =
253 |             condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
254 |         Node right = condition.getFirstChild();
255 |         if (outcome) {
256 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHEQ);
257 |         } else {
258 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHNE);
259 |         }
260 |     }
261 |     return nextPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
262 |         condition, blindScope, outcome);
263 |   }

/**
   * Given a constructor or an interface type, find out whether the unknown
   * type is a supertype of the current type.
   */
Method code:
1326 |   private static boolean hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType ctor) {
1327 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1328 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(!ctor.isUnknownType());
1329 | 
1330 |     // The type system should notice inheritance cycles on its own
1331 |     // and break the cycle.
1332 |     while (true) {
1333 |       ObjectType maybeSuperInstanceType =
1334 |           ctor.getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype();
1335 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType == null) {
1336 |         return false;
1337 |       }
1338 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType.isUnknownType() ||
1339 |           maybeSuperInstanceType.isEmptyType()) {
1340 |         return true;
1341 |       }
1342 |       ctor = maybeSuperInstanceType.getConstructor();
1343 |       if (ctor == null) {
1344 |         return false;
1345 |       }
1346 |       Preconditions.checkState(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1347 |     }
1348 |   }

===== M0093 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.getPrototype()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 366-388
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the {@code prototype} property of this function type. This is
   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.
   */
Method code:
366 |   public ObjectType getPrototype() {
367 |     // lazy initialization of the prototype field
368 |     if (prototypeSlot == null) {
369 |       String refName = getReferenceName();
370 |       if (refName == null) {
371 |         // Someone is trying to access the prototype of a structural function.
372 |         // We don't want to give real properties to this prototype, because
373 |         // then it would propagate to all structural functions.
374 |         setPrototypeNoCheck(
375 |            registry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE),
376 |            null);
377 |       } else {
378 |         setPrototype(
379 |             new PrototypeObjectType(
380 |                 registry,
381 |                 getReferenceName() + ".prototype",
382 |                 registry.getNativeObjectType(OBJECT_TYPE),
383 |                 isNativeObjectType(), null),
384 |             null);
385 |       }
386 |     }

/**
   * Extracts the text found on the current line starting at token. Note that

   * the token properly in the parser.

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.

   */

1314 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1315 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1316 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1317 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {

1319 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1320 |       } else {

1322 |       }
1323 |     }
1324 |     return builder.build();
1325 |   }

===== M0101 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,JSType,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 367-390
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param owner The owner of the property being assigned to.
   * @param propName The name of the property being assigned to.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
367 |   boolean expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
368 |       JSType leftType, Node owner, String propName) {
369 |     // The NoType check is a hack to make typedefs work OK.
370 |     if (!leftType.isNoType() && !rightType.isSubtype(leftType)) {
371 |       // Do not type-check interface methods, because we expect that
372 |       // they will have dummy implementations that do not match the type
373 |       // annotations.
374 |       JSType ownerType = getJSType(owner);
375 |       if (ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
376 |         FunctionType ownerFn = ownerType.toObjectType().getOwnerFunction();
377 |         if (ownerFn.isInterface() &&
378 |             rightType.isFunctionType() && leftType.isFunctionType()) {
379 |           return true;
380 |         }
381 |       }
382 | 
383 |       mismatch(t, n,
384 |           "assignment to property " + propName + " of " +
385 |           getReadableJSTypeName(owner, true),
386 |           rightType, leftType);
387 |       return false;
388 |     }
389 |     return true;
390 |   }

/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
308 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {

310 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
311 |     } else {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpressionList(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1779-1796
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpressionList := TopLevelTypeExpression
   *     | TopLevelTypeExpression ',' TypeExpressionList
   */
Method code:
1779 |   private Node parseTypeExpressionList(JsDocToken token) {
1780 |     Node typeExpr = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1781 |     if (typeExpr == null) {
1782 |       return null;
1783 |     }
1784 |     Node typeList = IR.block();
1785 |     typeList.addChildToBack(typeExpr);
1786 |     while (match(JsDocToken.COMMA)) {
1787 |       next();
1788 |       skipEOLs();
1789 |       typeExpr = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(next());
1790 |       if (typeExpr == null) {
1791 |         return null;
1792 |       }
1793 |       typeList.addChildToBack(typeExpr);
1794 |     }
1795 |     return typeList;
1796 |   }

===== M0143 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.allFlowSlots()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 346-362
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets all the symbols that have been defined before this point
   * in the current flow. Does not return slots that have not changed during
   * the flow.
   *
   * For example, consider the code:
   * <code>
   * var x = 3;
   * function f() {
   *   var y = 5;
   *   y = 6; // FLOW POINT
   *   var z = y;
   *   return z;
   * }
   * </code>
   * A FlowScope at FLOW POINT will return a slot for y, but not
   * a slot for x or z.
   */
Method code:
346 |   private Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> allFlowSlots() {
347 |     Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slots = Maps.newHashMap();
348 |     for (LinkedFlowSlot slot = lastSlot;
349 |          slot != null; slot = slot.parent) {
350 |       if (!slots.containsKey(slot.getName())) {
351 |         slots.put(slot.getName(), slot);
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     for (Map.Entry<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> symbolEntry : cache.symbols.entrySet()) {
356 |       if (!slots.containsKey(symbolEntry.getKey())) {
357 |         slots.put(symbolEntry.getKey(), symbolEntry.getValue());
358 |       }
359 |     }
360 | 
361 |     return slots;
362 |   }

===== M0145 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1730-1749
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the condition of an ON_TRUE / ON_FALSE CFG edge.
   * @param n a node with an outgoing conditional CFG edge
   * @return the condition node or null if the condition is not obviously a node
   */
Method code:
1730 |   static Node getConditionExpression(Node n) {
1731 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1732 |       case Token.IF:
1733 |       case Token.WHILE:
1734 |         return n.getFirstChild();
1735 |       case Token.DO:
1736 |         return n.getLastChild();
1737 |       case Token.FOR:
1738 |         switch (n.getChildCount()) {
1739 |           case 3:
1740 |             return null;
1741 |           case 4:
1742 |             return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
1743 |         }
1744 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("malformed 'for' statement " + n);
1745 |       case Token.CASE:
1746 |         return null;
1747 |     }
1748 |     throw new IllegalArgumentException(n + " does not have a condition.");
1749 |   }

===== M0164 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 830-857
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
830 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
831 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
832 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
833 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
834 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
835 |           return;
836 |         }
837 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
838 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
839 | 
840 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
841 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
842 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           } else {
844 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
845 |           }
846 |         } else { // Has a catch.
847 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
848 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
849 |             return;
850 |           } else {
851 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
852 |           }
853 |         }
854 |         lastJump = handler;
855 |       }
856 |     }
857 |   }

===== M0184 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.buildAndRegister()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 652-714
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.
   */
Method code:
652 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
653 |     if (returnType == null) {
654 |       // Infer return types.
655 |       // We need to be extremely conservative about this, because of two
656 |       // competing needs.
657 |       // 1) If we infer the return type of f too widely, then we won't be able
658 |       //    to assign f to other functions.
659 |       // 2) If we infer the return type of f too narrowly, then we won't be
660 |       //    able to override f in subclasses.
661 |       // So we only infer in cases where the user doesn't expect to write
662 |       // @return annotations--when it's very obvious that the function returns
663 |       // nothing.
664 |       if (!contents.mayHaveNonEmptyReturns() &&
665 |           !contents.mayHaveSingleThrow() &&
666 |           !contents.mayBeFromExterns()) {
667 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
668 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
669 |       }
670 |     }
671 | 
672 |     if (returnType == null) {
673 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
674 |     }
675 | 
676 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
677 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
678 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
679 |     }
680 | 
681 |     FunctionType fnType;
682 |     if (isConstructor) {
683 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
684 |     } else if (isInterface) {
685 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(
686 |           fnName, contents.getSourceNode(), classTemplateTypeNames);
687 |       if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
688 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
689 |       }
690 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
691 |     } else {
692 |       fnType = new FunctionBuilder(typeRegistry)
693 |           .withName(fnName)
694 |           .withSourceNode(contents.getSourceNode())
695 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
696 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
697 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
698 |           .withTemplateKeys(templateTypeNames)
699 |           .build();
700 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
701 |     }
702 | 
703 |     if (implementedInterfaces != null) {
704 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
705 |     }
706 | 
707 |     if (extendedInterfaces != null) {
708 |       fnType.setExtendedInterfaces(extendedInterfaces);
709 |     }
710 | 
711 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeNames();
712 | 
713 |     return fnType;
714 |   }

===== M0192 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInferencePass.inferAllScopes(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInferencePass.java
Original range: 77-112
Documentation:
/** Entry point for type inference when running over part of the tree. */
Method code:
77 |   void inferAllScopes(Node node) {
78 |     // Type analysis happens in two major phases.
79 |     // 1) Finding all the symbols.
80 |     // 2) Propagating all the inferred types.
81 |     //
82 |     // The order of this analysis is non-obvious. In a complete inference
83 |     // system, we may need to backtrack arbitrarily far. But the compile-time
84 |     // costs would be unacceptable.
85 |     //
86 |     // We do one pass where we do typed scope creation for all scopes
87 |     // in pre-order.
88 |     //
89 |     // Then we do a second pass where we do all type inference
90 |     // (type propagation) in pre-order.
91 |     //
92 |     // We use a memoized scope creator so that we never create a scope
93 |     // more than once.
94 |     //
95 |     // This will allow us to handle cases like:
96 |     // var ns = {};
97 |     // (function() { /** JSDoc */ ns.method = function() {}; })();
98 |     // ns.method();
99 |     // In this code, we need to build the symbol table for the inner scope in
100 |     // order to propagate the type of ns.method in the outer scope.
101 |     (new NodeTraversal(
102 |         compiler, new FirstScopeBuildingCallback(), scopeCreator))
103 |         .traverseWithScope(node, topScope);
104 | 
105 |     for (Scope s : scopeCreator.getAllMemoizedScopes()) {
106 |       s.resolveTypes();
107 |     }
108 | 
109 |     (new NodeTraversal(
110 |         compiler, new SecondScopeBuildingCallback(), scopeCreator))
111 |         .traverseWithScope(node, topScope);
112 |   }

===== M0209 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseHelperLoop(JsDocToken,List<ExtendedTypeInfo>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 226-271
Documentation:

Method code:
226 |   private boolean parseHelperLoop(JsDocToken token,
227 |                                   List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes) {
228 |     while (true) {
229 |       switch (token) {
230 |         case ANNOTATION:
231 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
232 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
233 |             token = parseAnnotation(token, extendedTypes);
234 |           } else {
235 |             token = next();
236 |           }
237 |           break;
238 | 
239 |         case EOC:
240 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
241 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
242 |           }
243 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
244 |           return true;
245 | 
246 |         case EOF:
247 |           // discard any accumulated information
248 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
249 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
250 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
252 |           return false;
253 | 
254 |         case EOL:
255 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
256 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
257 |           }
258 |           token = next();
259 |           break;
260 | 
261 |         default:
262 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
263 |             token = next();
264 |           } else {
265 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
266 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
267 |           }
268 |           break;
269 |       }
270 |     }
271 |   }

===== M0217 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1806-1849
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1806 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1807 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1808 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1809 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1810 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1811 |       // {?} - right curly
1812 |       // {?=} - equals
1813 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1814 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1815 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1816 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1817 |       // {Array.<?>} - greater than
1818 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1819 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1820 |       token = next();
1821 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1822 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1823 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1824 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1825 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1826 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE ||
1827 |           token == JsDocToken.GT) {
1828 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1829 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1830 |       }
1831 | 
1832 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1833 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1834 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1835 |     } else {
1836 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1837 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1838 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1839 |           next();
1840 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1841 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1842 |           next();
1843 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1844 |         }
1845 |       }
1846 | 
1847 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1848 |     }
1849 |   }

===== M0218 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createRecordTypeFromNodes(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1818-1869
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a RecordType from the nodes representing said record type.
   * @param n The node with type info.
   * @param sourceName The source file name.
   * @param scope A scope for doing type name lookups.
   */
Method code:
1818 |   private JSType createRecordTypeFromNodes(Node n, String sourceName,
1819 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1820 | 
1821 |     RecordTypeBuilder builder = new RecordTypeBuilder(this);
1822 | 
1823 |     // For each of the fields in the record type.
1824 |     for (Node fieldTypeNode = n.getFirstChild();
1825 |          fieldTypeNode != null;
1826 |          fieldTypeNode = fieldTypeNode.getNext()) {
1827 | 
1828 |       // Get the property's name.
1829 |       Node fieldNameNode = fieldTypeNode;
1830 |       boolean hasType = false;
1831 | 
1832 |       if (fieldTypeNode.getType() == Token.COLON) {
1833 |         fieldNameNode = fieldTypeNode.getFirstChild();
1834 |         hasType = true;
1835 |       }
1836 | 
1837 |       String fieldName = fieldNameNode.getString();
1838 | 
1839 |       // TODO(user): Move this into the lexer/parser.
1840 |       // Remove the string literal characters around a field name,
1841 |       // if any.
1842 |       if (fieldName.startsWith("'") || fieldName.startsWith("\"")) {
1843 |         fieldName = fieldName.substring(1, fieldName.length() - 1);
1844 |       }
1845 | 
1846 |       // Get the property's type.
1847 |       JSType fieldType = null;
1848 | 
1849 |       if (hasType) {
1850 |         // We have a declared type.
1851 |         fieldType = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1852 |             fieldTypeNode.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1853 |       } else {
1854 |         // Otherwise, the type is UNKNOWN.
1855 |         fieldType = getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1856 |       }
1857 | 
1858 |       // Add the property to the record.
1859 |       if (builder.addProperty(fieldName, fieldType, fieldNameNode) == null) {
1860 |         // Duplicate field name, warning and skip
1861 |         reporter.warning(
1862 |             "Duplicate record field " + fieldName,
1863 |             sourceName,
1864 |             n.getLineno(), fieldNameNode.getCharno());
1865 |       }
1866 |     }
1867 | 
1868 |     return builder.build();
1869 |   }

===== M0222 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.finishConstructorDefinition(Node,String,FunctionType,Scope,CompilerInput,Var)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1249-1293
Documentation:

Method code:
1249 |     private void finishConstructorDefinition(
1250 |         Node n, String variableName, FunctionType fnType,
1251 |         Scope scopeToDeclareIn, CompilerInput input, Var newVar) {
1252 |       // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1253 |       FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1254 |       Property prototypeSlot = fnType.getSlot("prototype");
1255 | 
1256 |       // When we declare the function prototype implicitly, we
1257 |       // want to make sure that the function and its prototype
1258 |       // are declared at the same node. We also want to make sure
1259 |       // that the if a symbol has both a Var and a JSType, they have
1260 |       // the same node.
1261 |       //
1262 |       // This consistency is helpful to users of SymbolTable,
1263 |       // because everything gets declared at the same place.
1264 |       prototypeSlot.setNode(n);
1265 | 
1266 |       String prototypeName = variableName + ".prototype";
1267 | 
1268 |       // There are some rare cases where the prototype will already
1269 |       // be declared. See TypedScopeCreatorTest#testBogusPrototypeInit.
1270 |       // Fortunately, other warnings will complain if this happens.
1271 |       Var prototypeVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(prototypeName);
1272 |       if (prototypeVar != null && prototypeVar.scope == scopeToDeclareIn) {
1273 |         scopeToDeclareIn.undeclare(prototypeVar);
1274 |       }
1275 | 
1276 |       scopeToDeclareIn.declare(prototypeName,
1277 |           n, prototypeSlot.getType(), input,
1278 |           /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1279 |           superClassCtor == null ||
1280 |           superClassCtor.getInstanceType().isEquivalentTo(
1281 |               getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1282 | 
1283 |       // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1284 |       // it constructs itself.
1285 |       if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1286 |           !n.isFromExterns()) {
1287 |         compiler.report(
1288 |             JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1289 |                 fnType.isConstructor() ?
1290 |                 CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1291 |                 variableName));
1292 |       }
1293 |     }

===== M0238 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 599-614
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
599 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
600 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
601 |       Set<String> directives = null;
602 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
603 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
604 |         if (directives == null) {
605 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
606 |         } else {
607 |           directives.add(directive);
608 |         }
609 |       }
610 | 
611 |       if (directives != null) {
612 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
613 |       }
614 |     }

===== M0242 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 796-810
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthrough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
796 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
797 |     switch (n.getType()) {
798 |       case Token.DO:
799 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
800 |       case Token.FOR:
801 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
802 |           return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
803 |         }
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
805 |       case Token.LABEL:

===== M0243 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.isQualifiedNameInferred(String,Node,JSDocInfo,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1660-1703
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a qualified name is inferred.
     * NOTE(nicksantos): Determining whether a property is declared or not
     * is really really obnoxious.
     *
     * The problem is that there are two (equally valid) coding styles:
     *
     * (function() {
     *   /* The authoritative definition of goog.bar. /
     *   goog.bar = function() {};
     * })();
     *
     * function f() {
     *   goog.bar();
     *   /* Reset goog.bar to a no-op. /
     *   goog.bar = function() {};
     * }
     *
     * In a dynamic language with first-class functions, it's very difficult
     * to know which one the user intended without looking at lots of
     * contextual information (the second example demonstrates a small case
     * of this, but there are some really pathological cases as well).
     *
     * The current algorithm checks if either the declaration has
     * JsDoc type information, or @const with a known type,
     * or a function literal with a name we haven't seen before.
     */
Method code:
1660 |     private boolean isQualifiedNameInferred(
1661 |         String qName, Node n, JSDocInfo info,
1662 |         Node rhsValue, JSType valueType) {
1663 |       if (valueType == null) {
1664 |         return true;
1665 |       }
1666 | 
1667 |       boolean inferred = true;
1668 |       if (info != null) {
1669 |         inferred = !(info.hasType()
1670 |             || info.hasEnumParameterType()
1671 |             || (isConstantSymbol(info, n) && valueType != null
1672 |                 && !valueType.isUnknownType())
1673 |             || FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1674 |       }
1675 | 
1676 |       if (inferred && rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isFunction()) {
1677 |         if (info != null) {
1678 |           return false;
1679 |         } else if (!scope.isDeclared(qName, false) &&
1680 |             n.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
1681 | 
1682 |           // Check if this is in a conditional block.
1683 |           // Functions assigned in conditional blocks are inferred.
1684 |           for (Node current = n.getParent();
1685 |                !(current.isScript() || current.isFunction());
1686 |                current = current.getParent()) {
1687 |             if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(current)) {
1688 |               return true;
1689 |             }
1690 |           }
1691 | 
1692 |           // Check if this is assigned in an inner scope.
1693 |           // Functions assigned in inner scopes are inferred.
1694 |           AstFunctionContents contents =
1695 |               getFunctionAnalysisResults(scope.getRootNode());
1696 |           if (contents == null ||
1697 |               !contents.getEscapedQualifiedNames().contains(qName)) {
1698 |             return false;
1699 |           }
1700 |         }
1701 |       }
1702 |       return inferred;
1703 |     }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 617-647
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
617 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
618 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
619 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
620 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
621 |     if (isOptional) {
622 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
623 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
624 |       // error can be reported.
625 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
626 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
627 |         emittedWarning = true;
628 |       }
629 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
630 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
631 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
632 |         emittedWarning = true;
633 |       }
634 |     } else {
635 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
636 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
637 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
638 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
639 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
640 |         } else {
641 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
642 |         }
643 |         emittedWarning = true;
644 |       }
645 |     }
646 |     return emittedWarning;
647 |   }

/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
515 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
516 |     int type = n.getType();
517 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
518 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
519 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
520 |     }
521 | 
522 |     curNode = n;
523 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) {
524 |       return;
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
528 |       traverseFunction(n, parent);
529 |     } else {
530 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
531 |         // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
532 |         // would no longer point to the true next
533 |         Node next = child.getNext();
534 |         traverseBranch(child, n);
535 |         child = next;
536 |       }
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     curNode = n;
540 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
541 |   }

273 |   public boolean isStruct() {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1724-1793
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1724 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1725 |     FunctionType functionType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(n.getJSType());
1726 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1727 |     if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1728 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1729 |       if (baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1730 |           baseConstructor != null &&
1731 |           baseConstructor.isInterface()) {
1732 |         compiler.report(
1733 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1734 |                         "constructor", functionPrivateName));
1735 |       } else {
1736 |         if (baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE)) {
1737 |           ObjectType proto = functionType.getPrototype();
1738 |           if (functionType.makesStructs() && !proto.isStruct()) {
1739 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_SHAPE_TYPE,
1740 |                                         "struct", functionPrivateName));
1741 |           } else if (functionType.makesDicts() && !proto.isDict()) {
1742 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_SHAPE_TYPE,
1743 |                                         "dict", functionPrivateName));
1744 |           }
1745 |         }
1746 |         // All interfaces are properly implemented by a class
1747 |         for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1748 |           boolean badImplementedType = false;
1749 |           ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1750 |           if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1751 |             FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1752 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1753 |             if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1754 |                 !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1755 |               badImplementedType = true;
1756 |             }
1757 |           } else {
1758 |             badImplementedType = true;
1759 |           }
1760 |           if (badImplementedType) {
1761 |             report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1762 |           }
1763 |         }
1764 |         // check properties
1765 |         validator.expectAllInterfaceProperties(t, n, functionType);
1766 |       }
1767 |     } else if (functionType.isInterface()) {
1768 |       // Interface must extend only interfaces
1769 |       for (ObjectType extInterface : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1770 |         if (extInterface.getConstructor() != null
1771 |             && !extInterface.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
1772 |           compiler.report(
1773 |               t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1774 |                           "interface", functionPrivateName));
1775 |         }
1776 |       }
1777 | 
1778 |       // Check whether the extended interfaces have any conflicts
1779 |       if (functionType.getExtendedInterfacesCount() > 1) {
1780 |         // Only check when extending more than one interfaces
1781 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> properties
1782 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1783 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> currentProperties
1784 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1785 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1786 |           currentProperties.clear();
1787 |           checkInterfaceConflictProperties(t, n, functionPrivateName,
1788 |               properties, currentProperties, interfaceType);
1789 |           properties.putAll(currentProperties);
1790 |         }
1791 |       }
1792 |     }
1793 |   }

/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:

676 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
677 |     }
678 | 
679 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
680 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
681 | 
682 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
683 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
684 |   }

361 |           continue;

385 |       }

===== M0303 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 258-308
Documentation:

Method code:
258 |   @Override
259 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
260 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
261 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
262 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
263 |         return true;
264 |       }
265 | 
266 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
267 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
268 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate through all
269 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
270 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
271 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
272 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
273 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
274 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
275 |         return false;
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
279 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
280 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
281 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
282 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
283 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
284 |             return false;
285 |           }
286 |         }
287 | 
288 |         return true;
289 |       }
290 | 
291 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
292 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
293 | 
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
299 |       }
300 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
301 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
302 |           return false;
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |       return true;
306 |     }
307 |     return false;
308 |   }

214 |     }
215 | 
216 |     // Top of the record types is the empty record, or OBJECT_TYPE.
217 |     if (registry.getNativeObjectType(
218 |             JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE).isSubtype(that)) {
219 |       return true;
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // A type is a subtype of a record type if it itself is a record
223 |     // type and it has at least the same members as the parent record type
224 |     // with the same types.
225 |     if (!that.isRecordType()) {
226 |       return false;
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
230 |   }

===== M0312 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 735-777
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
735 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
736 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
737 |         fnName, contents.getSourceNode(), parametersNode, returnType,
738 |         classTemplateTypeNames);
739 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
740 | 
741 |     if (makesStructs) {
742 |       fnType.setStruct();
743 |     } else if (makesDicts) {
744 |       fnType.setDict();
745 |     }
746 |     if (existingType != null) {
747 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType.isInstanceType();
748 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
749 |         FunctionType existingFn =
750 |             isInstanceObject ?
751 |             existingType.toObjectType().getConstructor() :
752 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
753 | 
754 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
755 |           existingFn.setSource(contents.getSourceNode());
756 |         }
757 | 
758 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
759 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, formatFnName(),
760 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
761 |         }
762 | 
763 |         return existingFn;
764 |       } else {
765 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
766 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
767 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
768 |       }
769 |     }
770 | 
771 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
772 | 
773 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
774 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
775 |     }
776 |     return fnType;
777 |   }

69 |   TemplateTypeMap(JSTypeRegistry registry,
70 |                   ImmutableList<TemplateType> templateKeys,
71 |                   ImmutableList<JSType> templateValues) {
72 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(templateKeys);

74 |

83 |     // of this TemplateTypeMap.

89 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if this map and the specified map have equivalent template
   * types.
   */
Method code:
196 |   public boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
197 |       TemplateTypeMap that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
198 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thisKeys = getTemplateKeys();
199 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thatKeys = that.getTemplateKeys();
200 | 
201 |     EquivalenceMatch[] thatMatches = new EquivalenceMatch[thatKeys.size()];
202 |     Arrays.fill(thatMatches, EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH);
203 | 
204 |     for (int i = 0; i < thisKeys.size(); i++) {
205 |       TemplateType thisKey = thisKeys.get(i);
206 |       JSType thisType = getResolvedTemplateType(thisKey);
207 |       EquivalenceMatch thisMatch = EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH;
208 | 
209 |       for (int j = 0; j < thatKeys.size(); j++) {
210 |         TemplateType thatKey = thatKeys.get(j);
211 |         JSType thatType = that.getResolvedTemplateType(thatKey);
212 | 
213 |         // Cross-compare every key-value pair in this TemplateTypeMap with
214 |         // those in that TemplateTypeMap. Update the Equivalence match for both
215 |         // key-value pairs involved.
216 |         if (thisKey == thatKey) {
217 |           EquivalenceMatch newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MISMATCH;
218 |           if (thisType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatType, eqMethod)) {
219 |             newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH;
220 |           }
221 | 
222 |           if (thisMatch != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
223 |             thisMatch = newMatchType;
224 |           }
225 |           if (thatMatches[j] != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
226 |             thatMatches[j] = newMatchType;
227 |           }
228 |         }
229 |       }
230 | 
231 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thisMatch, eqMethod)) {
232 |         return false;
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     for (int i = 0; i < thatMatches.length; i++) {
237 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thatMatches[i], eqMethod)) {
238 |         return false;
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     return true;
243 |   }
