===== M0318 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 159-205
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
159 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
160 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
161 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
162 |       case Token.BLOCK:
163 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
164 |       case Token.TRY:
165 |         return true;
166 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
167 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
168 |         // bleeds into the local scope and parameters are assigned
169 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
170 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
171 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
172 |         // control is going into the function's body, represented by the second
173 |         // child.
174 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
175 |       case Token.WHILE:
176 |       case Token.DO:
177 |       case Token.IF:
178 |         // These control structures are represented by a node that holds the
179 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
180 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.FOR:
183 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structures in that
184 |         // it has an initialization and an increment statement. Those
185 |         // two statements have corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
186 |         // The FOR node only represents the condition check for each iteration.
187 |         // That way the following:
188 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
189 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
190 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
191 |           // TODO(user): Investigate how we should handle the case where
192 |           // we have a very complex expression inside the FOR-IN header.
193 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
194 |         } else {
195 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
196 |         }
197 |       case Token.SWITCH:
198 |       case Token.CASE:
199 |       case Token.CATCH:
200 |       case Token.WITH:
201 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
202 |       default:
203 |         return false;
204 |     }
205 |   }

/**
   * Defines a property whose type is explicitly declared by the programmer.

===== M0320 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 567-615
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
567 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
568 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
569 |     Node obj = getprop.getFirstChild();
570 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(obj);
571 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
572 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
573 |     if (objectType == null) {
574 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
575 |     } else {
576 |       // Don't add the property to @struct objects outside a constructor
577 |       if (nodeType.isStruct() && !objectType.hasProperty(propName)) {
578 |         if (!(obj.isThis() &&
579 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor())) {
580 |           return;
581 |         }
582 |       }
583 | 
584 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
585 |         return;
586 |       }
587 | 
588 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
589 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
590 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
591 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the property.
592 |         //
593 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
594 |         //    inferred type.
595 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
596 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
597 |         //    define it.
598 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
599 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
600 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
601 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) || !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
602 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
603 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, rightType, getprop);
604 |           } else {
605 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, getprop);
606 |           }
607 |         } else if (obj.isThis() &&
608 |                    getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
609 |           objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, getprop);
610 |         } else {
611 |           registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
612 |         }
613 |       }
614 |     }
615 |   }

Method code:
81 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
82 |     try {
83 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
84 |       ParserRunner.ParseResult result = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
85 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
86 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
87 |           logger_);
88 |       root = result.ast;
89 |       compiler.setOldParseTree(sourceFile.getName(), result.oldAst);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
96 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
97 |       root = IR.script();
98 |     } else {
99 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
103 |     // the source file and module.
104 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
105 |   }

292 |   public int getMinArguments() {

===== M0345 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.FlatFlowScopeCache.FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope,LinkedFlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 423-487
Documentation:
// A cache at the join of two scope chains.
Method code:
423 |     FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeA,
424 |         LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeB) {
425 |       linkedEquivalent = null;
426 | 
427 |       // Always prefer the "real" function scope to the faked-out
428 |       // bottom scope.
429 |       functionScope = joinedScopeA.flowsFromBottom() ?
430 |           joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope() : joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope();
431 | 
432 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsA = joinedScopeA.allFlowSlots();
433 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsB = joinedScopeB.allFlowSlots();
434 | 
435 |       symbols = slotsA;
436 | 
437 |       // There are 5 different join cases:
438 |       // 1) The type is declared in joinedScopeA, not in joinedScopeB,
439 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in A.
440 |       // 2) The type is declared in joinedScopeB, not in joinedScopeA,
441 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in B.
442 |       // 3) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeA, but
443 |       //    not in joinedScopeB. Join the two types.
444 |       // 4) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeB, but
445 |       //    not in joinedScopeA. Join the two types.
446 |       // 5) The type is declared in joinedScopeA and joinedScopeB. Join
447 |       //    the two types.
448 |       Set<String> symbolNames = Sets.newHashSet(symbols.keySet());
449 |       symbolNames.addAll(slotsB.keySet());
450 | 
451 |       for (String name : symbolNames) {
452 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotA = slotsA.get(name);
453 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotB = slotsB.get(name);
454 | 
455 |         JSType joinedType = null;
456 |         if (slotB == null || slotB.getType() == null) {
457 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
458 |               = joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
459 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
460 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
461 |             // Case #1 -- already inserted.
462 |           } else {
463 |             // Case #3
464 |             joinedType = slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
465 |           }
466 |         } else if (slotA == null || slotA.getType() == null) {
467 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
468 |               = joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
469 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
470 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
471 |             // Case #2
472 |             symbols.put(name, slotB);
473 |           } else {
474 |             // Case #4
475 |             joinedType = slotB.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
476 |           }
477 |         } else {
478 |           // Case #5
479 |           joinedType =
480 |               slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(slotB.getType());
481 |         }
482 | 
483 |         if (joinedType != null) {
484 |           symbols.put(name, new SimpleSlot(name, joinedType, true));
485 |         }
486 |       }
487 |     }

===== M0355 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1229-1231
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1229 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1230 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1231 |   }

===== M0362 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 692-789
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
692 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
693 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
694 |     /*
695 |      * This is the case where:
696 |      *
697 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
698 |      * the script.
699 |      *
700 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
701 |      * the caller of the function.
702 |      *
703 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
704 |      * back to the caller of the function.
705 |      *
706 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
707 |      * asked to traverse.
708 |      *
709 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
710 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
711 |      */
712 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
713 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
714 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
715 |       return null;
716 |     }
717 | 
718 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
719 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
720 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
721 |       case Token.IF:
722 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
723 |       case Token.CASE:
724 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
725 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
726 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
727 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
728 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
730 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
731 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
732 |           } else {
733 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
734 |           }
735 |         } else {
736 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
737 |         }
738 |         break;
739 |       case Token.FOR:
740 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
741 |           return parent;
742 |         } else {
743 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
744 |         }
745 |       case Token.WHILE:
746 |       case Token.DO:
747 |         return parent;
748 |       case Token.TRY:
749 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
750 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
751 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
752 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
753 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
754 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
755 |           }
756 |         // CATCH block.
757 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
758 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
759 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
760 |           } else {
761 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
762 |           }
763 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
764 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
765 |           if (cfa != null) {
766 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
767 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.ON_EX, finallyNode);
768 |             }
769 |           }
770 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |         }
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
775 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
776 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
777 | 
778 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
779 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
780 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
784 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
785 |     } else {
786 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transferred up the AST.
787 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
788 |     }
789 |   }

===== M0364 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 484-523
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
485 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
486 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
487 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
488 |         parent.isTry() &&
489 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
490 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
491 |       return;
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
495 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
496 | 
497 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
498 |     // function (unless it is called)
499 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
500 |       child = child.getNext();
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     if (child != null) {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
505 |     } else {
506 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     // Synthetic blocks
510 |     if (parent != null) {
511 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
512 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
513 |         case Token.CASE:
514 |         case Token.TRY:
515 |           break;
516 |         default:
517 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
518 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
519 |           }
520 |           break;
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |   }

===== M0375 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1692-1727
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1692 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1693 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1694 | 
1695 |     skipEOLs();
1696 | 
1697 |     boolean restArg = false;
1698 |     token = next();
1699 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1700 |       token = next();
1701 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1702 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1703 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, IR.empty());
1704 |       }
1705 |       restArg = true;
1706 |     }
1707 | 
1708 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1709 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1710 |       skipEOLs();
1711 |       if (restArg) {
1712 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1713 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1714 |         next();
1715 |         skipEOLs();
1716 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1717 |       }
1718 | 
1719 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1720 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1721 |       } else {
1722 |         next();
1723 |       }
1724 |     }
1725 | 
1726 |     return typeNode;
1727 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(FunctionType,EquivalenceMethod)

Original range: 895-915
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
895 |   boolean checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
896 |       FunctionType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
897 |     if (isConstructor()) {
898 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
899 |         return this == that;
900 |       }
901 |       return false;

906 |       }
907 |       return false;
908 |     }

910 |       return false;
911 |     }
912 | 
913 |     return typeOfThis.checkEquivalenceHelper(that.typeOfThis, eqMethod) &&
914 |         call.checkArrowEquivalenceHelper(that.call, eqMethod);

===== M0388 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 519-562
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
519 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
520 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
521 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
522 |     switch (left.getType()) {
523 |       case Token.NAME:
524 |         String varName = left.getString();
525 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
526 | 
527 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
528 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
529 |         // This has two purposes:
530 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
531 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
532 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
533 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
534 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
535 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
536 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
537 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
538 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
539 |           redeclareSimpleVar(scope, left, resultType);
540 |         }
541 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
542 |             resultType : null);
543 | 
544 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
545 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
546 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
547 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
548 |         }
549 |         break;
550 |       case Token.GETPROP:
551 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
552 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
553 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(left, qualifiedName,
554 |               leftType == null ? unknownType : leftType,
555 |               resultType);
556 |         }
557 | 
558 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
559 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
560 |         break;
561 |     }
562 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,String,JSType)

Original range: 257-283
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect the type to be anything but the null or void type. If the
   * expectation is not met, issue a warning at the provided node's
   * source code position. Note that a union type that includes the
   * void type and at least one other type meets the expectation.
   * @return Whether the expectation was met.
   */
Method code:
257 |   boolean expectNotNullOrUndefined(
258 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, String msg, JSType expectedType) {
259 |     if (!type.isNoType() && !type.isUnknownType() &&
260 |         type.isSubtype(nullOrUndefined) &&
261 |         !containsForwardDeclaredUnresolvedName(type)) {
262 | 
263 |       // There's one edge case right now that we don't handle well, and
264 |       // that we don't want to warn about.
265 |       // if (this.x == null) {
266 |       //   this.initializeX();
267 |       //   this.x.foo();
268 |       // }
269 |       // In this case, we incorrectly type x because of how we
270 |       // infer properties locally. See issue 109.
271 |       // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=109
272 |       //
273 |       // We do not do this inference globally.
274 |       if (n.isGetProp() &&
275 |           !t.inGlobalScope() && type.isNullType()) {
276 |         return true;
277 |       }
278 | 
279 |       mismatch(t, n, msg, type, expectedType);
280 |       return false;
281 |     }
282 |     return true;
283 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 872-884
Documentation:
/**
   * Tests whether {@code this} and {@code that} are meaningfully
   * comparable using shallow comparison. By meaningfully, we mean compatible
   * types that are not rejected by step 1 of the definition of the Strict
   * Equality Comparison Algorithm (11.9.6, page 56&ndash;57) of the
   * ECMA-262 specification.<p>
   */
Method code:
872 |   public final boolean canTestForShallowEqualityWith(JSType that) {
873 |     if (isEmptyType() || that.isEmptyType()) {
874 |       return isSubtype(that) || that.isSubtype(this);
875 |     }
876 | 
877 |     JSType inf = getGreatestSubtype(that);
878 |     return !inf.isEmptyType() ||
879 |         // Our getGreatestSubtype relation on functions is pretty bad.
880 |         // Let's just say it's always ok to compare two functions.

882 |         // remove this.
883 |         inf == registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
884 |   }

===== M0413 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2477-2493
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2477 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2478 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2479 |     int c;
2480 |     while (true) {
2481 |       c = stream.getChar();
2482 |       if (c == ' ') {
2483 |         continue;
2484 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2485 |         matchedLc = true;
2486 |         break;
2487 |       } else {
2488 |         break;
2489 |       }
2490 |     }
2491 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2492 |     return matchedLc;
2493 |   }

===== M0421 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2055-2070
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is used as an L-value. Notice that sometimes
   * names are used as both L-values and R-values.
   *
   * We treat "var x;" as a pseudo-L-value, which kind of makes sense if you
   * treat it as "assignment to 'undefined' at the top of the scope". But if
   * we're honest with ourselves, it doesn't make sense, and we only do this
   * because it makes sense to treat this as syntactically similar to
   * "var x = 0;".
   *
   * @param n The node
   * @return True if n is an L-value.
   */
Method code:
2055 |   public static boolean isLValue(Node n) {
2056 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isName() || n.isGetProp() ||
2057 |         n.isGetElem());
2058 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
2059 |     if (parent == null) {
2060 |       return false;
2061 |     }
2062 |     return (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2063 |         || (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2064 |         || parent.isVar()
2065 |         || (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2066 |         || parent.isDec()
2067 |         || parent.isInc()
2068 |         || parent.isParamList()
2069 |         || parent.isCatch();
2070 |   }

===== M0425 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseNew(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1194-1230
Documentation:

Method code:
1194 |   private FlowScope traverseNew(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1195 |     scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
1196 | 
1197 |     Node constructor = n.getFirstChild();
1198 |     JSType constructorType = constructor.getJSType();
1199 |     JSType type = null;
1200 |     if (constructorType != null) {
1201 |       constructorType = constructorType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1202 |       if (constructorType.isUnknownType()) {
1203 |         type = unknownType;
1204 |       } else {
1205 |         FunctionType ct = constructorType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1206 |         if (ct == null && constructorType instanceof FunctionType) {
1207 |           // If constructorType is a NoObjectType, then toMaybeFunctionType will
1208 |           // return null. But NoObjectType implements the FunctionType
1209 |           // interface, precisely because it can validly construct objects.
1210 |           ct = (FunctionType) constructorType;
1211 |         }
1212 |         if (ct != null && ct.isConstructor()) {
1213 |           backwardsInferenceFromCallSite(n, ct);
1214 | 
1215 |           // If necessary, create a TemplatizedType wrapper around the instance
1216 |           // type, based on the types of the constructor parameters.
1217 |           ObjectType instanceType = ct.getInstanceType();
1218 |           Map<TemplateType, JSType> inferredTypes =
1219 |               inferTemplateTypesFromParameters(ct, n);
1220 |           if (inferredTypes.isEmpty()) {
1221 |             type = instanceType;
1222 |           } else {
1223 |             type = registry.createTemplatizedType(instanceType, inferredTypes);
1224 |           }
1225 |         }
1226 |       }
1227 |     }
1228 |     n.setJSType(type);
1229 |     return scope;
1230 |   }

===== M0444 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 912-1021
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.isAssign()</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
912 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
913 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
914 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
915 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
916 | 
917 |     // Check property sets to 'object.property' when 'object' is known.
918 |     if (lvalue.isGetProp()) {
919 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
920 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
921 |       Node property = lvalue.getLastChild();
922 |       String pname = property.getString();
923 | 
924 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
925 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
926 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
927 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
928 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
929 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
930 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, pname, lvalue, rvalue);
931 |         }
932 |       }
933 | 
934 |       checkEnumAlias(t, info, rvalue);
935 |       checkPropCreation(t, lvalue);
936 | 
937 |       // Prototype assignments are special, because they actually affect
938 |       // the definition of a class. These are mostly validated
939 |       // during TypedScopeCreator, and we only look for the "dumb" cases here.
940 |       // object.prototype = ...;
941 |       if (pname.equals("prototype")) {
942 |         if (objectJsType != null && objectJsType.isFunctionType()) {
943 |           FunctionType functionType = objectJsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
944 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
945 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
946 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
947 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
948 |             // Only assign structs to the prototype of a @struct constructor
949 |             if (functionType.makesStructs() && !rvalueType.isStruct()) {
950 |               String funName = functionType.getTypeOfThis().toString();
951 |               compiler.report(t.makeError(assign, CONFLICTING_SHAPE_TYPE,
952 |                                           "struct", funName));
953 |             }
954 |             return;
955 |           }
956 |         }
957 |       }
958 | 
959 |       // The generic checks for 'object.property' when 'object' is known,
960 |       // and 'property' is declared on it.
961 |       // object.property = ...;
962 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
963 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
964 |       if (type != null) {
965 |         if (type.hasProperty(pname) &&
966 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(pname) &&
967 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, pname)) {
968 |           JSType expectedType = type.getPropertyType(pname);
969 |           if (!expectedType.isUnknownType()) {
970 |             validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
971 |                 t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
972 |                 expectedType, object, pname);
973 |             checkPropertyInheritanceOnGetpropAssign(
974 |                 t, assign, object, pname, info, expectedType);
975 |             return;
976 |           }
977 |         }
978 |       }
979 | 
980 |       // If we couldn't get the property type with normal object property
981 |       // lookups, then check inheritance anyway with the unknown type.
982 |       checkPropertyInheritanceOnGetpropAssign(
983 |           t, assign, object, pname, info, getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
984 |     }
985 | 
986 |     // Check qualified name sets to 'object' and 'object.property'.
987 |     // This can sometimes handle cases when the type of 'object' is not known.
988 |     // e.g.,
989 |     // var obj = createUnknownType();
990 |     // /** @type {number} */ obj.foo = true;
991 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
992 |     if (lvalue.isQualifiedName()) {
993 |       // variable with inferred type case
994 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getQualifiedName());
995 |       if (var != null) {
996 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
997 |           return;
998 |         }
999 | 
1000 |         if (NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(lvalue).isThis() &&
1001 |             t.getScope() != var.getScope()) {
1002 |           // Don't look at "this.foo" variables from other scopes.
1003 |           return;
1004 |         }
1005 | 
1006 |         if (var.getType() != null) {
1007 |           leftType = var.getType();
1008 |         }
1009 |       }
1010 |     }
1011 | 
1012 |     // Fall through case for arbitrary LHS and arbitrary RHS.
1013 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
1014 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
1015 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1016 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
1017 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
1018 |     } else {
1019 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
1020 |     }
1021 |   }

===== M0453 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 325-416
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
325 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
326 |     if (info != null) {
327 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
328 |       makesStructs = info.makesStructs();
329 |       makesDicts = info.makesDicts();
330 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
331 | 
332 |       if (makesStructs && !isConstructor) {
333 |         reportWarning(CONSTRUCTOR_REQUIRED, "@struct", formatFnName());
334 |       } else if (makesDicts && !isConstructor) {
335 |         reportWarning(CONSTRUCTOR_REQUIRED, "@dict", formatFnName());
336 |       }
337 | 
338 |       // Class template types, which can be used in the scope of a constructor
339 |       // definition.
340 |       ImmutableList<String> typeParameters = info.getTemplateTypeNames();
341 |       if (!typeParameters.isEmpty()) {
342 |         if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
343 |           ImmutableList.Builder<TemplateType> builder = ImmutableList.builder();
344 |           for (String typeParameter : typeParameters) {
345 |             builder.add(typeRegistry.createTemplateType(typeParameter));
346 |           }
347 |           classTemplateTypeNames = builder.build();
348 |           typeRegistry.setTemplateTypeNames(classTemplateTypeNames);
349 |         }
350 |       }
351 | 
352 |       // base type
353 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
354 |         if (isConstructor) {
355 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
356 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
357 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
358 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
359 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
360 |           }
361 |         } else {
362 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, formatFnName());
363 |         }
364 |       }
365 | 
366 |       // Implemented interfaces (for constructors only).
367 |       if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
368 |         if (isConstructor) {
369 |           implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
370 |           Set<JSType> baseInterfaces = new HashSet<JSType>();
371 |           for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
372 |             JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
373 | 
374 |             if (maybeInterType != null &&
375 |                 maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
376 |               // Disallow implementing the same base (not templatized) interface
377 |               // type more than once.
378 |               JSType baseInterface = maybeInterType;
379 |               if (baseInterface.toMaybeTemplatizedType() != null) {
380 |                 baseInterface =
381 |                     baseInterface.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType();
382 |               }
383 |               if (baseInterfaces.contains(baseInterface)) {
384 |                 reportWarning(SAME_INTERFACE_MULTIPLE_IMPLEMENTS,
385 |                               baseInterface.toString());
386 |               } else {
387 |                 baseInterfaces.add(baseInterface);
388 |               }
389 | 
390 |               implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
391 |             }
392 |           }
393 |         } else if (isInterface) {
394 |           reportWarning(
395 |               TypeCheck.CONFLICTING_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, formatFnName());
396 |         } else {
397 |           reportWarning(CONSTRUCTOR_REQUIRED, "@implements", formatFnName());
398 |         }
399 |       }
400 | 
401 |       // extended interfaces (for interfaces only)
402 |       // We've already emitted a warning if this is not an interface.
403 |       if (isInterface) {
404 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
405 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
406 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
407 |           if (maybeInterfaceType != null &&
408 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
409 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
410 |           }
411 |         }
412 |       }
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     return this;
416 |   }

/**
   * FieldTypeList := FieldType | FieldType ',' FieldTypeList
   */
Method code:
2229 |   private Node parseFieldTypeList(JsDocToken token) {
2230 |     Node fieldTypeList = newNode(Token.LB);
2231 | 
2232 |     do {
2233 |       Node fieldType = parseFieldType(token);
2234 |

===== M0468 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 657-710
Documentation:

Method code:
657 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
658 |     String varName = n.getString();
659 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
660 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
661 |     if (value != null) {
662 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
663 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
664 |           getJSType(value));
665 |       return scope;
666 |     } else {
667 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
668 |       if (var != null) {
669 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
670 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
671 | 
672 |         // 1) The var is escaped and assigned in an inner scope, e.g.,
673 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
674 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
675 |         boolean unflowable = isInferred &&
676 |             isUnflowable(syntacticScope.getVar(varName));
677 | 
678 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
679 |         // inferred variable. That variable is assigned more than once,
680 |         // and we can't know which type we're getting.
681 |         //
682 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); } doStuff(); t = {};
683 |         //
684 |         // Notice that this heuristic isn't perfect. For example, you might
685 |         // have:
686 |         //
687 |         // function f() { (t); } f(); var t = 3;
688 |         //
689 |         // In this case, we would infer the first reference to t as
690 |         // type {number}, even though it's undefined.
691 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot = false;
692 |         if (isInferred && syntacticScope.isLocal()) {
693 |           Var maybeOuterVar = syntacticScope.getParent().getVar(varName);
694 |           if (var == maybeOuterVar &&
695 |               !maybeOuterVar.isMarkedAssignedExactlyOnce()) {
696 |             nonLocalInferredSlot = true;
697 |           }
698 |         }
699 | 
700 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
701 |           type = var.getType();
702 |           if (type == null) {
703 |             type = unknownType;
704 |           }
705 |         }
706 |       }
707 |     }
708 |     n.setJSType(type);
709 |     return scope;
710 |   }

/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1239 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {
1240 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1241 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1242 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno();
1243 | 
1244 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1245 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1246 |       int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1247 |       int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1248 | 
1249 |       jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno,
1250 |           endLineno, endCharno, true);
1251 |     }
1252 |     return typeNode;

===== M0490 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 2095-2146
Documentation:

Method code:
2095 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
2096 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
2097 |         return;
2098 |       }
2099 | 
2100 |       if (n.isReturn() && n.getFirstChild() != null) {
2101 |         data.get(t.getScopeRoot()).recordNonEmptyReturn();
2102 |       }
2103 | 
2104 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() <= 1) {
2105 |         // The first-order function analyzer looks at two types of variables:
2106 |         //
2107 |         // 1) Local variables that are assigned in inner scopes ("escaped vars")
2108 |         //
2109 |         // 2) Local variables that are assigned more than once.
2110 |         //
2111 |         // We treat all global variables as escaped by default, so there's
2112 |         // no reason to do this extra computation for them.
2113 |         return;
2114 |       }
2115 | 
2116 |       if (n.isName() && NodeUtil.isLValue(n) &&
2117 |           // Be careful of bleeding functions, which create variables
2118 |           // in the inner scope, not the scope where the name appears.
2119 |           !NodeUtil.isBleedingFunctionName(n)) {
2120 |         String name = n.getString();
2121 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
2122 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
2123 |         if (var != null) {
2124 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
2125 |           if (ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2126 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode()).recordAssignedName(name);
2127 |           }
2128 | 
2129 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2130 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode()).recordEscapedVarName(name);
2131 |           }
2132 |         }
2133 |       } else if (n.isGetProp() && n.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
2134 |           NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
2135 |         String name = NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(n).getString();
2136 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
2137 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
2138 |         if (var != null) {
2139 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
2140 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2141 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode())
2142 |                 .recordEscapedQualifiedName(n.getQualifiedName());
2143 |           }
2144 |         }
2145 |       }
2146 |     }

/**
   * <p>Checks enum aliases.
   *
   * <p>We verify that the enum element type of the enum used
   * for initialization is a subtype of the enum element type of
   * the enum the value is being copied in.</p>
   *
   * <p>Example:</p>
   * <pre>var myEnum = myOtherEnum;</pre>
   *
   * <p>Enum aliases are irregular, so we need special code for this :(</p>

/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.

1357 |       }
1358 |       return resolveResult;

1362 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);

===== M0512 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1954-1956
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1954 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1955 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1956 |   }

===== M0523 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 83-118
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   private void scanRoot(Node n) {
84 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
85 |       if (inputId == null) {
86 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
87 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
88 |         // from the AST.
89 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
90 |       }
91 | 
92 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
93 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
94 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
95 | 
96 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
97 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
98 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
99 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
100 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
101 |       }
102 | 
103 |       // Args: Declare function variables
104 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
105 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
106 |            a = a.getNext()) {
107 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
108 |         declareVar(a);
109 |       }
110 | 
111 |       // Body
112 |       scanVars(body);
113 |     } else {
114 |       // It's the global block
115 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
116 |       scanVars(n);
117 |     }
118 |   }

1397 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1398 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1399 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1400 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1401 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1402 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1403 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1404 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1405 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1406 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1407 |         parentNodeType == Token.PARAM_LIST ||
1408 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1409 |       return false;
1410 |     }
1411 | 
1412 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1413 |     if (type == null) {
1414 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1415 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1416 |       if (var != null) {
1417 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1418 |         if (varType != null) {
1419 |           type = varType;
1420 |         }
1421 |       }
1422 |     }
1423 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1424 |     return true;
1425 |   }

===== M0557 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

   * Creates a JsDocInfoParser and parses the JsDoc string.

===== M0561 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.RecordType.isSubtype(ObjectType,RecordType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/RecordType.java
Original range: 233-274
Documentation:
/** Determines if typeA is a subtype of typeB */
Method code:
233 |   static boolean isSubtype(ObjectType typeA, RecordType typeB) {
234 |     // typeA is a subtype of record type typeB iff:
235 |     // 1) typeA has all the properties declared in typeB.
236 |     // 2) And for each property of typeB,
237 |     //    2a) if the property of typeA is declared, it must be equal
238 |     //        to the type of the property of typeB,
239 |     //    2b) otherwise, it must be a subtype of the property of typeB.
240 |     //
241 |     // To figure out why this is true, consider the following pseudo-code:
242 |     // /** @type {{a: (Object,null)}} */ var x;
243 |     // /** @type {{a: !Object}} */ var y;
244 |     // var z = {a: {}};
245 |     // x.a = null;
246 |     //
247 |     // y cannot be assigned to x, because line 4 would violate y's declared
248 |     // properties. But z can be assigned to x. Even though z and y are the
249 |     // same type, the properties of z are inferred--and so an assignment
250 |     // to the property of z would not violate any restrictions on it.
251 |     for (String property : typeB.getOwnPropertyNames()) {
252 |       if (!typeA.hasProperty(property)) {
253 |         return false;
254 |       }
255 | 
256 |       JSType propA = typeA.getPropertyType(property);
257 |       JSType propB = typeB.getPropertyType(property);
258 |       if (typeA.isPropertyTypeDeclared(property)) {
259 |         // If one declared property isn't invariant,
260 |         // then the whole record isn't covariant.
261 |         if (!propA.isInvariant(propB)) {
262 |           return false;
263 |         }
264 |       } else {
265 |         // If one inferred property isn't a subtype,
266 |         // then the whole record isn't covariant.
267 |         if (!propA.isSubtype(propB)) {
268 |           return false;
269 |         }
270 |       }
271 |     }
272 | 
273 |     return true;
274 |   }

===== M0573 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo,AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 289-344
Documentation:

Method code:
289 |   private void validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo info, AstNode node) {
290 |     if (info.hasType()) {
291 |       boolean valid = false;
292 |       switch (node.getType()) {
293 |         // Casts are valid
294 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.LP:
295 |           valid = node instanceof ParenthesizedExpression;
296 |           break;
297 |         // Variable declarations are valid
298 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.VAR:
299 |           valid = true;
300 |           break;
301 |         // Function declarations are valid
302 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.FUNCTION:
303 |           FunctionNode fnNode = (FunctionNode) node;
304 |           valid = fnNode.getFunctionType() == FunctionNode.FUNCTION_STATEMENT;
305 |           break;
306 |         // Object literal properties and catch declarations are valid.
307 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NAME:
308 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty
309 |               || node.getParent() instanceof CatchClause
310 |               || node.getParent() instanceof FunctionNode;
311 |           break;
312 |         // Object literal properties are valid
313 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GET:
314 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.SET:
315 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NUMBER:
316 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.STRING:
317 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty;
318 |           break;
319 | 
320 |         // Property assignments are valid, if at the root of an expression.
321 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.ASSIGN:
322 |           if (node instanceof Assignment) {
323 |             valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent())
324 |                 && isPropAccess(((Assignment) node).getLeft());
325 |           }
326 |           break;
327 | 
328 |         // Property definitions are valid, if at the root of an expression.
329 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETPROP:
330 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETELEM:
331 |           valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent());
332 |           break;
333 | 
334 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.CALL:
335 |           valid = info.isDefine();
336 |           break;
337 |       }
338 |       if (!valid) {
339 |         errorReporter.warning(MISPLACED_TYPE_ANNOTATION,
340 |             sourceName,
341 |             node.getLineno(), "", 0);
342 |       }
343 |     }
344 |   }

===== M0576 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 294-355
Documentation:

Method code:
294 |   @Override
295 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
296 |     switch (n.getType()) {
297 |       case Token.IF:
298 |         handleIf(n);
299 |         return;
300 |       case Token.WHILE:
301 |         handleWhile(n);
302 |         return;
303 |       case Token.DO:
304 |         handleDo(n);
305 |         return;
306 |       case Token.FOR:
307 |         handleFor(n);
308 |         return;

===== M0579 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseFieldType(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2260-2288
Documentation:
/**
   * FieldType := FieldName | FieldName ':' TypeExpression
   */
Method code:
2260 |   private Node parseFieldType(JsDocToken token) {
2261 |     Node fieldName = parseFieldName(token);
2262 | 
2263 |     if (fieldName == null) {
2264 |       return null;
2265 |     }
2266 | 
2267 |     skipEOLs();
2268 |     if (!match(JsDocToken.COLON)) {
2269 |       return fieldName;
2270 |     }
2271 | 
2272 |     // Move to the colon.
2273 |     next();
2274 | 
2275 |     // Move to the token after the colon and parse
2276 |     // the type expression.
2277 |     skipEOLs();
2278 |     Node typeExpression = parseTypeExpression(next());
2279 | 
2280 |     if (typeExpression == null) {
2281 |       return null;
2282 |     }
2283 | 
2284 |     Node fieldType = newNode(Token.COLON);
2285 |     fieldType.addChildToBack(fieldName);
2286 |     fieldType.addChildToBack(typeExpression);
2287 |     return fieldType;
2288 |   }

120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.BITAND:
125 |       case Token.BITOR:
126 |       case Token.BITXOR:
127 |       case Token.COMMA:
128 |       case Token.DIV:
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.GE:
131 |       case Token.GT:
132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

305 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
306 |     switch (n.getType()) {
307 |       case Token.ASSIGN:

309 |         break;
310 | 
311 |       case Token.NAME:

315 |       case Token.GETPROP:
316 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);

318 |

326 |             .createChildFlowScope();
327 |         break;

331 |         break;
332 | 
333 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
334 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
335 |         break;
336 | 
337 |       case Token.CALL:
338 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
339 |         break;
340 | 
341 |       case Token.NEW:
342 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
343 |         break;
344 | 
345 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
346 |       case Token.ADD:
347 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
348 |         break;
349 | 
350 |       case Token.POS:
351 |       case Token.NEG:
352 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
353 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
354 |         break;
355 | 
356 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
357 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
358 |         break;

361 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
362 |         break;
363 |

366 |       case Token.LSH:
367 |       case Token.RSH:
368 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
369 |       case Token.URSH:
370 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
371 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
372 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
373 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
374 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
375 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
376 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
377 |       case Token.DIV:
378 |       case Token.MOD:
379 |       case Token.BITAND:
380 |       case Token.BITXOR:
381 |       case Token.BITOR:
382 |       case Token.MUL:
383 |       case Token.SUB:
384 |       case Token.DEC:
385 |       case Token.INC:
386 |       case Token.BITNOT:
387 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
388 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
389 |         break;
390 | 
391 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
392 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
393 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
394 |         break;
395 | 
396 |       case Token.COMMA:
397 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
398 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
399 |         break;
400 | 
401 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
402 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
403 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
404 |         break;
405 | 
406 |       case Token.DELPROP:
407 |       case Token.LT:
408 |       case Token.LE:
409 |       case Token.GT:
410 |       case Token.GE:
411 |       case Token.NOT:
412 |       case Token.EQ:
413 |       case Token.NE:
414 |       case Token.SHEQ:
415 |       case Token.SHNE:
416 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
417 |       case Token.IN:
418 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
419 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
420 |         break;

423 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
424 |         break;
425 | 
426 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
427 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
428 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isGetProp()) {
429 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());

431 |         break;
432 |

434 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
435 |         break;
436 | 
437 |       case Token.RETURN:
438 |         scope = traverseReturn(n, scope);
439 |         break;
440 | 
441 |       case Token.VAR:
442 |       case Token.THROW:
443 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
444 |         break;
445 | 
446 |       case Token.CATCH:
447 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
448 |         break;
449 | 
450 |       case Token.CAST:
451 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
452 |         JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
453 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
454 |           n.setJSType(info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry));
455 |         }
456 |         break;
457 |     }

460 |   }

/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.

     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.

     */
Method code:
1150 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1151 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1152 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1153 | 
1154 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.isName() && scope.isGlobal();
1155 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1156 |           isGlobalVar &&
1157 |           (parent.isVar() ||
1158 |            parent.isFunction());
1159 | 
1160 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1161 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1162 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1163 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1164 |       if (n.isGetProp() && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1165 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1166 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1167 | 
1168 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1169 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1170 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1171 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1172 |         }
1173 |       }
1174 | 
1175 |       // The input may be null if we are working with a AST snippet. So read
1176 |       // the extern info from the node.
1177 |       Var newVar = null;
1178 | 
1179 |       // declared in closest scope?
1180 |       CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(inputId);
1181 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1182 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1183 |         newVar = validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1184 |             sourceName, input, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1185 |       } else {
1186 |         if (type != null) {
1187 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1188 |         }
1189 | 
1190 |         newVar =
1191 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1192 | 
1193 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1194 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1195 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1196 |               (initialValue.isObjectLit() ||
1197 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1198 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1199 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1200 |           }
1201 |         }
1202 |       }
1203 | 
1204 |       // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1205 |       FunctionType fnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(type);
1206 |       if (fnType != null &&
1207 |           // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1208 |           !type.isEmptyType()) {
1209 | 
1210 |         // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance type
1211 |         // (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1212 |         // This doesn't apply to structural constructors (like
1213 |         // function(new:Array). Checking the constructed type against
1214 |         // the variable name is a sufficient check for this.
1215 |         if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1216 |             variableName.equals(fnType.getReferenceName())) {
1217 |           finishConstructorDefinition(n, variableName, fnType, scopeToDeclareIn,
1218 |                                       input, newVar);
1219 |         }
1220 |       }
1221 | 
1222 |       if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1223 |         ObjectType globalThis =
1224 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1225 |         if (inferred) {
1226 |           globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1227 |               type == null ?
1228 |               getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1229 |               type,
1230 |               n);
1231 |         } else {
1232 |           globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, n);
1233 |         }
1234 |       }
1235 | 
1236 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1237 |           && type != null
1238 |           && type.isFunctionType()
1239 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1240 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1241 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();

1246 |       }
1247 |     }

===== M0624 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1855-1881
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1855 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1856 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1857 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1858 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1859 |       skipEOLs();
1860 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1861 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1862 |       skipEOLs();
1863 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1864 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1865 |       skipEOLs();
1866 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1867 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1868 |       String string = stream.getString();
1869 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1870 |         skipEOLs();
1871 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1872 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1873 |         return newStringNode(string);
1874 |       } else {
1875 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1876 |       }
1877 |     }
1878 | 
1879 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1880 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1881 |   }
