===== M0630 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 190-303
Documentation:

Method code:
190 |   @Override
191 |   @SuppressWarnings({"fallthrough", "incomplete-switch"})
192 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
193 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
194 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
195 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
196 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably too expensive to be
197 |     // worthwhile.
198 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
199 |     Node condition = null;
200 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
201 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
202 | 
203 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
204 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
205 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
206 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
207 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
208 | 
209 |       switch (branch) {
210 |         case ON_TRUE:
211 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
212 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
213 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
214 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
215 | 
216 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
217 | 
218 |             if (item.isVar()) {
219 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
220 |             }
221 |             if (item.isName()) {
222 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
223 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
224 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
225 |                   null : objType.getTemplateTypeMap().getTemplateType(
226 |                       registry.getObjectIndexKey());
227 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
228 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
229 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
230 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
231 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
232 |                 }
233 |               }
234 |               redeclareSimpleVar(informed, item, iterKeyType);
235 |             }
236 |             newScope = informed;
237 |             break;
238 |           }
239 | 
240 |           // FALL THROUGH
241 | 
242 |         case ON_FALSE:
243 |           if (condition == null) {
244 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
245 |             if (condition == null && source.isCase()) {
246 |               condition = source;
247 | 
248 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
249 |               // of the loop.
250 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
251 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
252 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
253 |               }
254 |             }
255 |           }
256 | 
257 |           if (condition != null) {
258 |             if (condition.isAnd() ||
259 |                 condition.isOr()) {
260 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
261 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
262 |               // scope on false.
263 |               //
264 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
265 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
266 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
267 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
268 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
269 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
270 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
271 | 
272 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
273 |               // of the loop.
274 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
275 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.isAnd() ?
276 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
277 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
278 |               }
279 |               newScope =
280 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
281 |                       condition,
282 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
283 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
284 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
285 |             } else {
286 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
287 |               // of the loop.
288 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
289 |                 conditionFlowScope =
290 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
291 |               }
292 |               newScope =
293 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
294 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
295 |             }
296 |           }
297 |           break;
298 |       }
299 | 
300 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
301 |     }
302 |     return result;
303 |   }

===== M0631 =====

===== M0639 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 249-307
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
249 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
250 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
251 |     if (oldType == null) {
252 |       return this;
253 |     }
254 | 
255 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
256 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
257 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
258 |       // Not a function literal.
259 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
260 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
261 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
262 |       }
263 |     } else {
264 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
265 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
266 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
267 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
268 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
269 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
270 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
271 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
272 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
273 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
274 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
275 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
276 | 
277 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
278 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
279 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
280 | 
281 |           // The subclass method might write its var_args as individual
282 |           // arguments.
283 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
284 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
285 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
286 |           }
287 |         } else {
288 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
289 |               paramBuilder,
290 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
291 |               warnedAboutArgList,
292 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
293 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
294 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
295 |         }
296 |       }
297 | 
298 |       // Clone any remaining params that aren't in the function literal,
299 |       // but make them optional.
300 |       while (oldParams.hasNext()) {
301 |         paramBuilder.newOptionalParameterFromNode(oldParams.next());
302 |       }
303 | 
304 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
305 |     }
306 |     return this;
307 |   }

===== M0642 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.getGreatestSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 951-1003
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common
   * getGreatestSubtype implementations.
   */
Method code:
951 |   static JSType getGreatestSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
952 |     if (thisType.isFunctionType() && thatType.isFunctionType()) {
953 |       // The FunctionType sub-lattice is not well-defined. i.e., the
954 |       // proposition
955 |       // A < B => sup(A, B) == B
956 |       // does not hold because of unknown parameters and return types.
957 |       // See the comment in supAndInfHelper for more info on this.
958 |       return thisType.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(
959 |           thatType.toMaybeFunctionType(), false);
960 |     } else if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
961 |       return thisType;
962 |     } else if (thisType.isUnknownType() || thatType.isUnknownType()) {
963 |       // The greatest subtype with any unknown type is the universal
964 |       // unknown type, unless the two types are equal.
965 |       return thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType) ? thisType :
966 |           thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
967 |     } else if (thisType.isUnionType()) {
968 |       return thisType.toMaybeUnionType().meet(thatType);
969 |     } else if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
970 |       return thatType.toMaybeUnionType().meet(thisType);
971 |     } else if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
972 |       return thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getGreatestSubtypeHelper(
973 |           thatType);
974 |     }  else if (thatType.isTemplatizedType()) {
975 |       return thatType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getGreatestSubtypeHelper(
976 |           thisType);
977 |     } else if (thisType.isSubtype(thatType)) {
978 |       return filterNoResolvedType(thisType);
979 |     } else if (thatType.isSubtype(thisType)) {
980 |       return filterNoResolvedType(thatType);
981 |     } else if (thisType.isRecordType()) {
982 |       return thisType.toMaybeRecordType().getGreatestSubtypeHelper(thatType);
983 |     } else if (thatType.isRecordType()) {
984 |       return thatType.toMaybeRecordType().getGreatestSubtypeHelper(thisType);
985 |     }
986 | 
987 |     if (thisType.isEnumElementType()) {
988 |       JSType inf = thisType.toMaybeEnumElementType().meet(thatType);
989 |       if (inf != null) {
990 |         return inf;
991 |       }
992 |     } else if (thatType.isEnumElementType()) {
993 |       JSType inf = thatType.toMaybeEnumElementType().meet(thisType);
994 |       if (inf != null) {
995 |         return inf;
996 |       }
997 |     }
998 | 
999 |     if (thisType.isObject() && thatType.isObject()) {
1000 |       return thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_OBJECT_TYPE);
1001 |     }
1002 |     return thisType.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE);
1003 |   }

201 |   private void attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
201 |   boolean parse() {
202 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
203 |     skipEOLs();
204 | 
205 |     JsDocToken token = next();
206 | 
207 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
208 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
209 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
210 |       token = blockInfo.token;
211 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
212 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
213 |       }
214 |     } else {
215 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
216 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
217 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
218 |         // what it was.
219 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
220 |       }
221 |     }
222 | 
223 |     return parseHelperLoop(token, Lists.<ExtendedTypeInfo>newArrayList());
224 |   }

/**
   * Computes the subset of {@code this} and {@code that} types under

1177 |   public TypePair getTypesUnderShallowInequality(JSType that) {
1178 |     // union types

===== M0682 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 2086-2105
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
2086 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
2087 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
2088 |     Preconditions.checkState(!n.isFunction() ||
2089 |             type.isFunctionType() ||
2090 |             type.isUnknownType());
2091 |     // TODO(johnlenz): this seems like a strange place to check "@implicitCast"
2092 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
2093 |     if (info != null) {
2094 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
2095 |         String propName = n.isGetProp() ?
2096 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
2097 |         compiler.report(
2098 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
2099 |       }
2100 |     }
2101 | 
2102 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
2103 |       n.setJSType(type);
2104 |     }
2105 |   }

===== M0686 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3064-3092
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3064 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3065 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3066 |     if (info == null) {
3067 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3068 |       if (parent == null) {
3069 |         return null;
3070 |       }
3071 | 
3072 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3073 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3074 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3075 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3076 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent)) {
3077 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3078 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3079 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3080 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3081 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3082 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3083 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3084 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3085 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3086 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3087 |       } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3088 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3089 |       }
3090 |     }
3091 |     return info;
3092 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
112 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
113 |                                   String sourceString,
114 |                                   Config config,
115 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
116 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
117 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
118 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
119 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
120 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
121 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
122 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
123 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
124 | 
125 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
126 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
127 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
128 | 
129 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
130 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
131 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
132 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
133 | 
134 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
135 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
136 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
137 | 
138 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
139 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
140 |     try {
141 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
142 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
143 |       logger.info(
144 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
145 |     } finally {
146 |       Context.exit();
147 |     }
148 |     Node root = null;
149 |     if (astRoot != null) {
150 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
151 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
152 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
153 |     }
154 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
155 |   }

===== M0715 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 483-512
Documentation:

Method code:
483 |     @Override
484 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
485 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
486 |       if (n.isFunction() ||
487 |           n.isScript()) {
488 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
489 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
490 |       }
491 | 
492 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
493 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
494 |       boolean descend = parent == null || !parent.isFunction() ||
495 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
496 | 
497 |       if (descend) {
498 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
499 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.
500 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementParent(n)) {
501 |           for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
502 |                child != null;
503 |                child = child.getNext()) {
504 |             if (NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(child)) {
505 |               defineFunctionLiteral(child);
506 |             }
507 |           }
508 |         }
509 |       }
510 | 
511 |       return descend;
512 |     }

===== M0719 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0724 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // templatized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isTemplatizedType() || current.isTemplatizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isTemplatizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isTemplatizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isTemplatizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isTemplatizedType());
224 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
225 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedCurrent = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (templatizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(templatizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   if (alternate.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
229 |                       current.getTemplateTypeMap(),
230 |                       EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the templatized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = templatizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both templatized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
794 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
795 |     if (item == null) {
796 |       return null;
797 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
798 |       return item.getNext();
799 |     } else {
800 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
801 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {

803 |       } else {
804 |         return item;
805 |       }
806 |     }
807 |   }

===== M0731 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 288-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 |   }

===== M0752 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1887-1922
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   */
Method code:
1887 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1888 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1889 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1890 |     }
1891 | 
1892 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1893 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1894 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1895 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1896 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1897 |       skipEOLs();
1898 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1899 |         next();
1900 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1901 |       }
1902 |     }
1903 | 
1904 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1905 | 
1906 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1907 |       next();
1908 |       skipEOLs();
1909 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1910 |       if (memberType != null) {
1911 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1912 | 
1913 |         skipEOLs();
1914 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1915 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1916 |         }
1917 | 
1918 |         next();
1919 |       }
1920 |     }
1921 |     return typeNameNode;
1922 |   }

===== M0756 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1524-1631
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1524 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1525 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1526 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1527 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1528 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1529 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1530 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1531 | 
1532 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1533 |       // 1) @type annotation / @enum annotation
1534 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1535 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1536 |       // 4) ASSIGN to something marked @const
1537 |       // 5) ASSIGN to anything else
1538 |       //
1539 |       // 1, 3, and 4 are declarations, 5 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1540 |       // the function has JsDoc or has not been declared before.
1541 |       //
1542 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1543 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1544 | 
1545 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3 + #4
1546 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredType(info, n, rhsValue);
1547 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1548 |         // Determining type for #5
1549 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1550 |       }
1551 | 
1552 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1553 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1554 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1555 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1556 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1557 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1558 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1559 |         if (qVar != null) {
1560 |           // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1561 |           // and they assign F.prototype to an object literal,
1562 |           // then they are responsible for making sure that the object literal's
1563 |           // implicit prototype is set up appropriately. We just obey
1564 |           // the @extends tag.
1565 |           ObjectType qVarType = ObjectType.cast(qVar.getType());
1566 |           if (qVarType != null &&
1567 |               rhsValue != null &&
1568 |               rhsValue.isObjectLit()) {
1569 |             typeRegistry.resetImplicitPrototype(
1570 |                 rhsValue.getJSType(), qVarType.getImplicitPrototype());
1571 |           } else if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1572 |             // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1573 |             // and they assign F.prototype to some arbitrary expression,
1574 |             // there's not much we can do. We just ignore the expression,
1575 |             // and hope they've annotated their code in a way to tell us
1576 |             // what props are going to be on that prototype.
1577 |             return;
1578 |           }
1579 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1580 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1581 |           }
1582 |         }
1583 |       }
1584 | 
1585 |       if (valueType == null) {
1586 |         if (parent.isExprResult()) {
1587 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1588 |               n,
1589 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1590 |               ownerName));
1591 |         }
1592 | 
1593 |         return;
1594 |       }
1595 | 
1596 |       boolean inferred = isQualifiedNameInferred(
1597 |           qName, n, info, rhsValue, valueType);
1598 |       if (!inferred) {
1599 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1600 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1601 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1602 |           // declared yet.
1603 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1604 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1605 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1606 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1607 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1608 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1609 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, n);
1610 |           }
1611 |         }
1612 | 
1613 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1614 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1615 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1616 |       } else if (rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isTrue()) {
1617 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1618 |         FunctionType ownerType =
1619 |             JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(getObjectSlot(ownerName));
1620 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1621 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ownerType.getTypeOfThis();
1622 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1623 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1624 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1625 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1626 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1627 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE), true);
1628 |           }
1629 |         }
1630 |       }
1631 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkPropertyAccess(JSType,String,NodeTraversal,Node)

Original range: 1460-1488
Documentation:
/**
   * Emit a warning if we can prove that a property cannot possibly be
   * defined on an object. Note the difference between JS and a strictly
   * statically typed language: we're checking if the property
   * *cannot be defined*, whereas a java compiler would check if the
   * property *can be undefined*.
   */
Method code:
1460 |   private void checkPropertyAccess(JSType childType, String propName,
1461 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1462 |     // If the property type is unknown, check the object type to see if it
1463 |     // can ever be defined. We explicitly exclude CHECKED_UNKNOWN (for
1464 |     // properties where we've checked that it exists, or for properties on
1465 |     // objects that aren't in this binary).
1466 |     JSType propType = getJSType(n);
1467 |     if (propType.isEquivalentTo(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1468 |       childType = childType.autobox();
1469 |       ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(childType);
1470 |       if (objectType != null) {
1471 |         // We special-case object types so that checks on enums can be
1472 |         // much stricter, and so that we can use hasProperty (which is much
1473 |         // faster in most cases).
1474 |         if (!objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
1475 |             objectType.isEquivalentTo(
1476 |                 typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1477 |           if (objectType instanceof EnumType) {
1478 |             report(t, n, INEXISTENT_ENUM_ELEMENT, propName);
1479 |           } else {
1480 |             checkPropertyAccessHelper(objectType, propName, t, n);
1481 |           }
1482 |         }
1483 | 
1484 |       } else {
1485 |         checkPropertyAccessHelper(childType, propName, t, n);
1486 |       }
1487 |     }
1488 |   }

===== M0765 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 200-259
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
200 |   @Override
201 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
202 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
203 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
204 |     // show up in the type registry.
205 |     Scope newScope = null;
206 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
207 |     if (parent == null) {
208 |       JSType globalThis =
209 |           typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
210 | 
211 |       // Mark the main root, the externs root, and the src root
212 |       // with the global this type.
213 |       root.setJSType(globalThis);
214 |       root.getFirstChild().setJSType(globalThis);
215 |       root.getLastChild().setJSType(globalThis);
216 | 
217 |       // Run a first-order analysis over the syntax tree.
218 |       (new FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer(compiler, functionAnalysisResults))
219 |           .process(root.getFirstChild(), root.getLastChild());
220 | 
221 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
222 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
223 | 
224 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
225 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
226 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
227 |     } else {
228 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
229 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
230 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
231 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
235 | 
236 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
237 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
238 |     // build properties on.
239 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
240 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
241 |       if (type != null && type.isFunctionType()) {
242 |         FunctionType fnType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
243 |         JSType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
244 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
245 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
246 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
247 |         }
248 |       }
249 |     }
250 | 
251 |     if (parent == null) {
252 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
253 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes,
254 |           delegateCallingConventions);
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     newScope.setTypeResolver(scopeBuilder);
258 |     return newScope;
259 |   }

===== M0768 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 1014-1054
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   @Override
1015 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1016 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 | 
1020 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
1021 |       FunctionType other = that.toMaybeFunctionType();
1022 |       if (other.isInterface()) {
1023 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
1024 |         return true;
1025 |       }
1026 |       if (isInterface()) {
1027 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
1028 |         return false;
1029 |       }
1030 | 
1031 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
1032 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
1033 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
1034 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
1035 |       // this as an error. Let's punt on all this for now.
1036 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
1037 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
1038 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
1039 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
1040 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
1041 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
1042 |         (other.typeOfThis.toObjectType() != null &&
1043 |              other.typeOfThis.toObjectType().getConstructor() != null &&
1044 |              other.typeOfThis.toObjectType().getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
1045 | 
1046 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
1047 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
1048 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
1049 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
1050 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
1051 |     }
1052 | 
1053 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
1054 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredType(JSDocInfo,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1325-1378
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1325 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(JSDocInfo info, Node lValue,
1326 |         @Nullable Node rValue) {
1327 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1328 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(lValue, info);
1329 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.isFunction() &&
1330 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1331 |               JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(rValue.getJSType()), info, lValue)) {
1332 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1333 |       } else if (info != null) {
1334 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1335 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.isObjectLit()) {
1336 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1337 |           } else {
1338 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1339 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1340 |           }
1341 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1342 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1343 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1344 |         }
1345 |       }
1346 | 
1347 |       // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1348 |       if (isConstantSymbol(info, lValue)) {
1349 |         if (rValue != null) {
1350 |           JSDocInfo rValueInfo = rValue.getJSDocInfo();
1351 |           if (rValueInfo != null && rValueInfo.hasType()) {
1352 |             // If rValue has a type-cast, we use the type in the type-cast.
1353 |             return rValueInfo.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
1354 |           } else if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1355 |               && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1356 |             // If rValue's type was already computed during scope creation,
1357 |             // then we can safely use that.
1358 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1359 |           } else if (rValue.isOr()) {
1360 |             // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1361 |             // var x = x || TYPE;
1362 |             // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1363 |             // reasons.
1364 |             Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1365 |             Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1366 |             boolean namesMatch = firstClause.isName()
1367 |                 && lValue.isName()
1368 |                 && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1369 |             if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1370 |                 && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1371 |               return secondClause.getJSType();
1372 |             }
1373 |           }
1374 |         }
1375 |       }
1376 | 
1377 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(lValue, info);
1378 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 552-572
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
552 |   @Override
553 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
554 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
555 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
556 |     if (unknown) {
557 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
558 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
559 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
560 |         unknown = false;
561 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
562 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
563 |             unknown = true;
564 |             break;
565 |           }
566 |         }
567 |       } else {
568 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
569 |       }
570 |     }
571 |     return unknown;
572 |   }

122 |   private void inferArguments(Scope functionScope) {
123 |     Node functionNode = functionScope.getRootNode();
124 |     Node astParameters = functionNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
125 |     Node iifeArgumentNode = null;
126 | 
127 |     if (NodeUtil.isCallOrNewTarget(functionNode)) {
128 |       iifeArgumentNode = functionNode.getNext();

132 |         JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(functionNode.getJSType());
133 |     if (functionType != null) {
134 |       Node parameterTypes = functionType.getParametersNode();
135 |       if (parameterTypes != null) {
136 |         Node parameterTypeNode = parameterTypes.getFirstChild();
137 |         for (Node astParameter : astParameters.children()) {
138 |           Var var = functionScope.getVar(astParameter.getString());
139 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(var);

141 |               var.getType() == unknownType) {
142 |             JSType newType = null;
143 |

146 |             } else if (parameterTypeNode != null) {

148 |             }
149 |

151 |               var.setType(newType);

153 |             }

157 |             parameterTypeNode = parameterTypeNode.getNext();
158 |           }
159 |           if (iifeArgumentNode != null) {
160 |             iifeArgumentNode = iifeArgumentNode.getNext();
161 |           }
162 |         }
163 |       }
164 |     }
165 |   }

61 |     for (;;) {
62 |       // eat white spaces
63 |       for (;;) {
64 |         charno = -1;
65 |         c = getChar();
66 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
67 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
68 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
70 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
71 |           break;
72 |         }
73 |       }
74 | 
75 |       switch (c) {
76 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
77 |         case '@':
78 |           do {
79 |             c = getChar();
80 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
81 |               addToString(c);
82 |             } else {
83 |               ungetChar(c);
84 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
85 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
86 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
87 |             }
88 |           } while (true);
89 | 
90 |         case '*':
91 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
92 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
93 |           } else {
94 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
95 |           }
96 | 
97 |         case ',':
98 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
99 | 
100 |         case '>':
101 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
102 | 
103 |         case '(':
104 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
105 | 
106 |         case ')':
107 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
108 | 
109 |         case '{':
110 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
111 | 
112 |         case '}':
113 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
114 | 
115 |         case '[':
116 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
117 | 
118 |         case ']':
119 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
120 | 
121 |         case '?':
122 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
123 | 
124 |         case '!':
125 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
126 | 
127 |         case ':':
128 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
129 | 
130 |         case '=':
131 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
132 | 
133 |         case '|':
134 |           matchChar('|');
135 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
136 | 
137 |         case '.':
138 |           c = getChar();
139 |           if (c == '<') {
140 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
141 |           } else {
142 |             if (c == '.') {
143 |               c = getChar();
144 |               if (c == '.') {
145 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
146 |               } else {
147 |                 addToString('.');
148 |               }
149 |             }
150 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
151 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
152 |             c = '.';
153 |           }
154 |           // fall through
155 | 
156 |         default: {
157 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
158 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
159 |           int c1 = c;
160 |           addToString(c);
161 |           int c2 = getChar();
162 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
163 |             ungetChar(c2);
164 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
165 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
166 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
167 |           } else {
168 |             do {
169 |               c1 = c2;
170 |               c2 = getChar();
171 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
172 |                 ungetChar(c2);
173 |                 ungetChar(c1);
174 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
175 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
176 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
177 |               } else {
178 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
179 |                   addToString(c1);
180 |                 } else {
181 |                   ungetChar(c2);
182 |                   addToString(c1);
183 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
184 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
185 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
186 |                 }
187 |               }
188 |             } while (true);
189 |           }
190 |         }
191 |       }
192 |     }
193 |   }

===== M0819 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.reconcileOptionsWithGuards()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 313-352
Documentation:
/**
   * When the CompilerOptions and its WarningsGuard overlap, reconcile
   * any discrepencies.
   */
Method code:
313 |   protected void reconcileOptionsWithGuards() {
314 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
315 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
316 |       options.checkTypes = true;
317 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
318 |       options.checkTypes = false;
319 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
320 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
321 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
322 |       // parser type warnings.
323 |       options.setWarningLevel(
324 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
325 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
326 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
330 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
331 |       options.setWarningLevel(
332 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
333 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
337 |       options.setWarningLevel(
338 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
339 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
340 |     }
341 | 
342 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
343 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
344 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
345 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
346 |     // error off.
347 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
348 |         !options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
349 |       options.setWarningLevel(
350 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF);
351 |     }
352 |   }

===== M0832 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

/**
   * Returns whether a node corresponds to a simple or a qualified name, such as
   * <code>x</code> or <code>a.b.c</code> or <code>this.a</code>.
   */

===== M0854 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 298-321
Documentation:

Method code:
298 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
299 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
300 |       return;
301 |     }
302 | 
303 |     try {
304 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
305 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
306 | 
307 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
308 |       sourceName = "";
309 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
310 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
311 | 
312 |       for (Node root : roots) {
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
314 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
315 |       }
316 | 
317 |       popScope();
318 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
319 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
320 |     }
321 |   }

===== M0860 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseModifiesTag(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1087-1126
Documentation:
/**
   * Parse a {@code @modifies} tag of the form
   * {@code @modifies&#123;this|arguments|param&#125;}.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   */
Method code:
1087 |   private JsDocToken parseModifiesTag(JsDocToken token) {
1088 |     if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1089 |       Set<String> modifies = new HashSet<String>();
1090 |       while (true) {
1091 |         if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1092 |           String name = stream.getString();
1093 |           if (!modifiesAnnotationKeywords.contains(name)
1094 |               && !jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
1095 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies.unknown", name,
1096 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1097 |           }
1098 | 
1099 |           modifies.add(stream.getString());
1100 |           token = next();
1101 |         } else {
1102 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies",
1103 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1104 |           return token;
1105 |         }
1106 | 
1107 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1108 |           token = next();
1109 |         } else {
1110 |           break;
1111 |         }
1112 |       }
1113 | 
1114 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1115 |         parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies",
1116 |             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1117 |       } else {
1118 |         token = next();
1119 |         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordModifies(modifies)) {
1120 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies.duplicate",
1121 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1122 |         }
1123 |       }
1124 |     }
1125 |     return token;
1126 |   }

===== M0871 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 514-571
Documentation:

Method code:
514 |     @Override
515 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
516 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
517 |       attachLiteralTypes(n);
518 | 
519 |       switch (n.getType()) {
520 |         case Token.CALL:
521 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n);
522 |           checkForCallingConventionDefiningCalls(n, delegateCallingConventions);
523 |           break;
524 | 
525 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
526 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
527 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
528 |           }
529 | 
530 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
531 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
532 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n);
533 |           }
534 |           break;
535 | 
536 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
537 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
538 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
539 |           if (firstChild.isGetProp() &&
540 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
541 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
542 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
543 |           }
544 |           break;
545 | 
546 |         case Token.CATCH:
547 |           defineCatch(n);
548 |           break;
549 | 
550 |         case Token.VAR:
551 |           defineVar(n);
552 |           break;
553 | 
554 |         case Token.GETPROP:
555 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
556 |           if (parent.isExprResult() &&
557 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
558 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
559 |           }
560 |           break;
561 |       }
562 | 
563 |       // Analyze any @lends object literals in this statement.
564 |       if (n.getParent() != null && NodeUtil.isStatement(n) &&
565 |           lentObjectLiterals != null) {
566 |         for (Node objLit : lentObjectLiterals) {
567 |           defineObjectLiteral(objLit);
568 |         }
569 |         lentObjectLiterals.clear();
570 |       }
571 |     }

   * Visits a GETPROP node.

   * @param n The node being visited.

1435 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1436 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()

===== M0892 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ModificationVisitor.caseFunctionType(FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ModificationVisitor.java
Original range: 87-138
Documentation:

Method code:
87 |   @Override
88 |   public JSType caseFunctionType(FunctionType type) {
89 |     if (isNativeFunctionType(type)) {
90 |       return type;
91 |     }
92 | 
93 |     // TODO(johnlenz): remove this simplifying assumption...
94 |     if (!type.isOrdinaryFunction()) {
95 |       return type;
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     boolean changed = false;
99 | 
100 |     JSType beforeThis = type.getTypeOfThis();
101 |     JSType afterThis = coerseToThisType(beforeThis.visit(this));
102 |     if (beforeThis != afterThis) {
103 |       changed = true;
104 |     }
105 | 
106 |     JSType beforeReturn = type.getReturnType();
107 |     JSType afterReturn = beforeReturn.visit(this);
108 |     if (beforeReturn != afterReturn) {
109 |       changed = true;
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(registry);
113 |     for (Node paramNode : type.getParameters()) {
114 |       JSType beforeParamType = paramNode.getJSType();
115 |       JSType afterParamType = beforeParamType.visit(this);
116 |       if (beforeParamType != afterParamType) {
117 |         changed = true;
118 |       }
119 |       if (paramNode.isOptionalArg()) {
120 |         paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(afterParamType);
121 |       } else if (paramNode.isVarArgs()) {
122 |         paramBuilder.addVarArgs(afterParamType);
123 |       } else {
124 |         paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(afterParamType);
125 |       }
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     if (changed) {
129 |       // TODO(johnlenz): should we support preserving template keys?
130 |       FunctionBuilder builder = new FunctionBuilder(registry);
131 |       builder.withParams(paramBuilder);
132 |       builder.withReturnType(afterReturn);
133 |       builder.withTypeOfThis(afterThis);
134 |       return builder.build();
135 |     }
136 | 
137 |     return type;
138 |   }

===== M0898 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 544-577
Documentation:
/** Traverses a function. */
Method code:
544 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
545 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
546 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
547 | 
548 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
549 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
550 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
551 | 
552 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
553 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
554 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     curNode = n;
558 |     pushScope(n);
559 | 
560 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
561 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
562 |       // scope.
563 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
564 |     }
565 | 
566 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
567 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
568 | 
569 |     // Args
570 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
571 | 
572 |     // Body
573 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null && body.isBlock(), body);
574 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
575 | 
576 |     popScope();

/**
   * Creates a record type.
   *
   * @param registry The type registry under which this type lives.
   * @param properties A map of all the properties of this record type.
   * @param declared Whether this is a declared or synthesized type.
   *     A synthesized record type is just used for bookkeeping
   *     in the type system. A declared record type was actually used in the
   *     user's program.
   * @throws IllegalStateException if the {@code RecordProperty} associated
   *         with a property is null.
   */
Method code:
83 |   RecordType(JSTypeRegistry registry, Map<String, RecordProperty> properties,
84 |       boolean declared) {
85 |     super(registry, null, null);
86 |     setPrettyPrint(true);
87 |     this.declared = declared;
88 | 
89 |     for (String property : properties.keySet()) {
90 |       RecordProperty prop = properties.get(property);
91 |       if (prop == null) {
92 |         throw new IllegalStateException(
93 |             "RecordProperty associated with a property should not be null!");
94 |       }
95 |       if (declared) {
96 |         defineDeclaredProperty(
97 |             property, prop.getType(), prop.getPropertyNode());
98 |       } else {
99 |         defineSynthesizedProperty(
100 |             property, prop.getType(), prop.getPropertyNode());
101 |       }
102 |     }
103 | 
104 |     // Freeze the record type.
105 |     isFrozen = true;
106 |   }
