Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1637-1810
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1637 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1638 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1639 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1640 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1641 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1642 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1643 | 
1644 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1645 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1646 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1647 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1648 | 
1649 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1650 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1651 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1652 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1653 |         }
1654 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1655 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1656 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1657 | 
1658 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1659 |         return createOptionalType(
1660 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1661 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1662 | 
1663 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1664 |         return createOptionalType(
1665 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1666 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1667 | 
1668 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1669 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1670 | 
1671 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1672 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1673 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1674 | 
1675 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1676 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1677 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1678 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1679 |           builder.addAlternate(
1680 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1681 |         }
1682 |         return builder.build();
1683 | 
1684 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1685 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1686 | 
1687 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1688 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1689 | 
1690 |       case Token.STRING:
1691 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1692 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1693 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1694 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1695 |         }
1696 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1697 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1698 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1699 |           int nAllowedTypes =
1700 |               namedType.getTemplateTypeMap().numUnfilledTemplateKeys();
1701 |           if (typeList != null && nAllowedTypes > 0) {
1702 |             // Templatized types.
1703 |             ImmutableList.Builder<JSType> templateTypes =
1704 |                 ImmutableList.builder();
1705 | 
1706 |             // Special case for Object, where Object.<X> implies Object.<?,X>.
1707 |             if (n.getString().equals("Object") &&
1708 |                 typeList.getFirstChild() == typeList.getLastChild()) {
1709 |               templateTypes.add(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1710 |             }
1711 | 
1712 |             int templateNodeIndex = 0;
1713 |             for (Node templateNode : typeList.getFirstChild().siblings()) {
1714 |               // Don't parse more templatized type nodes than the type can
1715 |               // accommodate. This is because some existing clients have
1716 |               // template annotations on non-templatized classes, for instance:
1717 |               //   goog.structs.Set.<SomeType>
1718 |               // The problem in these cases is that the previously-unparsed
1719 |               // SomeType is not actually a valid type. To prevent these clients
1720 |               // from seeing unknown type errors, we explicitly don't parse
1721 |               // these types.
1722 |               // TODO(user): Address this issue by removing bad template
1723 |               // annotations on non-templatized classes.
1724 |               if (++templateNodeIndex > nAllowedTypes) {
1725 |                 break;
1726 |               }
1727 |               templateTypes.add(createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1728 |                   templateNode, sourceName, scope));
1729 |             }
1730 |             namedType = createTemplatizedType(
1731 |                 (ObjectType) namedType, templateTypes.build());
1732 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(namedType);
1733 |           }
1734 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1735 |         } else {
1736 |           return namedType;
1737 |         }
1738 | 
1739 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1740 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1741 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1742 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1743 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1744 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1745 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1746 |           thisType =
1747 |               ObjectType.cast(
1748 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1749 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1750 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1751 |           if (thisType == null) {
1752 |             reporter.warning(
1753 |                 SimpleErrorReporter.getMessage0(
1754 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1755 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1756 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1757 |                 sourceName,
1758 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), contextNode.getCharno());
1759 |           }
1760 | 
1761 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1762 |           current = current.getNext();
1763 |         }
1764 | 
1765 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1766 | 
1767 |         if (current.getType() == Token.PARAM_LIST) {
1768 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1769 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1770 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1771 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1772 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1773 |               } else {
1774 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1775 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1776 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1777 |               }
1778 |             } else {
1779 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1780 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1781 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1782 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1783 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1784 |                   reporter.warning(
1785 |                       SimpleErrorReporter.getMessage0(
1786 |                           "msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1787 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), arg.getCharno());
1788 |                 }
1789 |               } else {
1790 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1791 |               }
1792 |             }
1793 |           }
1794 |           current = current.getNext();
1795 |         }
1796 | 
1797 |         JSType returnType =
1798 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1799 | 
1800 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1801 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1802 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1803 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1804 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1805 |             .build();
1806 |     }
1807 | 
1808 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1809 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1810 |   }

===== M0909 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 92-189
Documentation:

Method code:
92 |   @Override
93 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
94 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
95 | 
96 |     switch (n.getType()) {
97 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
98 |       case Token.NAME:
99 |         if (parent == null) {
100 |           return;
101 |         }
102 | 
103 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
104 |         if (!parent.isVar() &&
105 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
106 |             !(parent.isAssign() &&
107 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
108 |           return;
109 |         }
110 | 
111 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
112 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
113 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
114 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
115 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
116 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
117 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
118 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
119 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
120 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
121 |         if (docInfo == null &&
122 |             !(parent.isVar() &&
123 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
124 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
125 |         }
126 | 
127 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
128 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
129 |         if (varType == null && parent.isFunction()) {
130 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
134 |         // we can do.
135 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
136 |           return;
137 |         }
138 | 
139 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
140 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
141 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
142 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
143 |           return;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
147 |         break;
148 | 
149 |       case Token.GETPROP:
150 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
151 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
152 |         //
153 |         // 1)
154 |         // /** @deprecated */
155 |         // obj.prop = ...
156 |         //
157 |         // 2)
158 |         // /** @deprecated */
159 |         // obj.prop;
160 |         if (parent.isExprResult() ||
161 |             (parent.isAssign() &&
162 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
163 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
164 |           if (docInfo == null) {
165 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
166 |           }
167 |           if (docInfo != null) {
168 |             ObjectType lhsType =
169 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
170 |             if (lhsType != null) {
171 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
172 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
173 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
174 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo);
175 |               }
176 | 
177 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
178 |               ObjectType propType =
179 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
180 |               if (propType != null) {
181 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
182 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
183 |               }
184 |             }
185 |           }
186 |         }
187 |         break;
188 |     }
189 |   }

505 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(

896 |             // Record types for @type.
897 |             // If the @private, @protected, or @public annotations
898 |             // have a type attached, pretend that they actually wrote:
899 |             // @type {type}\n@private
900 |             // This will have some weird behavior in some cases
901 |             // (for example, @private can now be used as a type-cast),
902 |             // but should be mostly OK.
903 |             if ((type != null && isAlternateTypeAnnotation)
904 |                 || annotation == Annotation.TYPE) {
905 |               if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
906 |                 parser.addTypeWarning(
907 |                     "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
908 |               }
909 |             }
910 | 
911 |             switch (annotation) {
912 |               case CONSTANT:
913 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
914 |                   parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
915 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
916 |                 }
917 |                 break;
918 | 
919 |               case DEFINE:
920 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
921 |                   parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
922 |                       lineno, charno);
923 |                 }
924 |                 return recordDescription(token);
925 | 
926 |               case PRIVATE:
927 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
928 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
929 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
930 |                       lineno, charno);
931 |                 }
932 |                 return recordDescription(token);
933 | 
934 |               case PROTECTED:
935 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
936 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
937 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
938 |                       lineno, charno);
939 |                 }
940 |                 return recordDescription(token);
941 | 
942 |               case PUBLIC:
943 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
944 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
945 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
946 |                       lineno, charno);
947 |                 }
948 |                 return recordDescription(token);
949 | 
950 |               case RETURN:
951 |                 if (type == null) {
952 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(newNode(Token.QMARK));
953 |                 }
954 | 
955 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
956 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
957 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
958 |                   break;
959 |                 }
960 | 
961 |                 // Find the return's description (if applicable).
962 |                 if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
963 |                   ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
964 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
965 | 
966 |                   String returnDescription =
967 |                       returnDescriptionInfo.string;
968 | 
969 |                   if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
970 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
971 |                         returnDescription);
972 |                   }
973 | 
974 |                   token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
975 |                 } else {
976 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
977 |                 }
978 |                 return token;
979 | 
980 |               case THIS:
981 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
982 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
983 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
984 |                 }
985 |                 break;
986 | 
987 |               case TYPEDEF:
988 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
989 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
990 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
991 |                 }
992 |                 break;
993 |             }
994 |           }
995 | 
996 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
997 |       }
998 |     }
999 | 
1000 |     return next();
1001 |   }

===== M0935 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.filterNoResolvedType(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1013-1040
Documentation:
/**
   * When computing infima, we may get a situation like
   * inf(Type1, Type2)
   * where both types are unresolved, so they're technically
   * subtypes of one another.
   *
   * If this happens, filter them down to NoResolvedType.
   */
Method code:
1013 |   static JSType filterNoResolvedType(JSType type) {
1014 |     if (type.isNoResolvedType()) {
1015 |       // inf(UnresolvedType1, UnresolvedType2) needs to resolve
1016 |       // to the base unresolved type, so that the relation is symmetric.
1017 |       return type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE);
1018 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
1019 |       UnionType unionType = type.toMaybeUnionType();
1020 |       boolean needsFiltering = false;
1021 |       for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
1022 |         if (alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
1023 |           needsFiltering = true;
1024 |           break;
1025 |         }
1026 |       }
1027 | 
1028 |       if (needsFiltering) {
1029 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(type.registry);
1030 |         builder.addAlternate(type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE));
1031 |         for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
1032 |           if (!alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
1033 |             builder.addAlternate(alt);
1034 |           }
1035 |         }
1036 |         return builder.build();
1037 |       }
1038 |     }
1039 |     return type;
1040 |   }

===== M0939 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 571-639
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
571 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
572 |     if (this == that) {
573 |       return true;
574 |     }
575 | 
576 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
577 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
578 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
579 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
580 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
581 |         // with everyone.
582 |         return true;
583 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
584 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
585 |         // both unknown.
586 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
587 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
588 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
589 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
590 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
591 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
592 |         return false;
593 |       }
594 |     }
595 | 
596 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
597 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
598 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
599 |     }
600 | 
601 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
602 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
603 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
607 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
608 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
609 |     }
610 | 
611 |     if (!getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
612 |         that.getTemplateTypeMap(), eqMethod)) {
613 |       return false;
614 |     }
615 | 
616 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
617 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
618 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
619 |     }
620 | 
621 |     // Unbox other proxies.
622 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
623 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
624 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
625 |               that, eqMethod);
626 |     }
627 | 
628 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
629 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
630 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
631 |           eqMethod);
632 |     }
633 | 
634 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
635 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
636 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
637 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
638 |     return this == that;
639 |   }

/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.

   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.

===== M0953 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
495 |   @Override
496 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
497 |     JSType childType;
498 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
499 |     Node left, right;
500 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
501 |     boolean typeable = true;
502 | 
503 |     switch (n.getType()) {
504 |       case Token.CAST:
505 |         Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
506 |         JSType exprType = getJSType(expr);
507 |         JSType castType = getJSType(n);
508 | 
509 |         // TODO(johnlenz): determine if we can limit object literals in some
510 |         // way.
511 |         if (!expr.isObjectLit()) {
512 |           validator.expectCanCast(t, n, castType, exprType);
513 |         }
514 |         ensureTyped(t, n, castType);
515 | 
516 |         if (castType.isSubtype(exprType) || expr.isObjectLit()) {
517 |           expr.setJSType(castType);
518 |         }
519 |         break;
520 | 
521 |       case Token.NAME:
522 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
523 |         break;
524 | 
525 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
526 |         typeable = false;
527 |         break;
528 | 
529 |       case Token.COMMA:
530 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.TRUE:
534 |       case Token.FALSE:
535 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
536 |         break;
537 | 
538 |       case Token.THIS:
539 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
540 |         break;
541 | 
542 |       case Token.NULL:
543 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
544 |         break;
545 | 
546 |       case Token.NUMBER:
547 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
548 |         break;
549 | 
550 |       case Token.STRING:
551 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
552 |         break;
553 | 
554 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
555 |         typeable = false;
556 |         break;
557 | 
558 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
559 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
560 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
561 |         break;
562 | 
563 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
564 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
565 |         break;
566 | 
567 |       case Token.REGEXP:
568 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.GETPROP:
572 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
573 |         typeable = !(parent.isAssign() &&
574 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
575 |         break;
576 | 
577 |       case Token.GETELEM:
578 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
579 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
580 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
581 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
582 |         typeable = false;
583 |         break;
584 | 
585 |       case Token.VAR:
586 |         visitVar(t, n);
587 |         typeable = false;
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.NEW:
591 |         visitNew(t, n);
592 |         break;
593 | 
594 |       case Token.CALL:
595 |         visitCall(t, n);
596 |         typeable = !parent.isExprResult();
597 |         break;
598 | 
599 |       case Token.RETURN:
600 |         visitReturn(t, n);
601 |         typeable = false;
602 |         break;
603 | 
604 |       case Token.DEC:
605 |       case Token.INC:
606 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
607 |         checkPropCreation(t, left);
608 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
609 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
610 |         break;
611 | 
612 |       case Token.NOT:
613 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
614 |         break;
615 | 
616 |       case Token.VOID:
617 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
618 |         break;
619 | 
620 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
621 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
622 |         break;
623 | 
624 |       case Token.BITNOT:
625 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
626 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
627 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
628 |               childType.toString());
629 |         }
630 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
631 |         break;
632 | 
633 |       case Token.POS:
634 |       case Token.NEG:
635 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
636 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
637 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
638 |         break;
639 | 
640 |       case Token.EQ:
641 |       case Token.NE:
642 |       case Token.SHEQ:
643 |       case Token.SHNE: {
644 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
645 |         right = n.getLastChild();
646 | 
647 |         if (left.isTypeOf()) {
648 |           if (right.isString()) {
649 |             checkTypeofString(t, right, right.getString());
650 |           }
651 |         } else if (right.isTypeOf() && left.isString()) {
652 |           checkTypeofString(t, left, left.getString());
653 |         }
654 | 
655 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
656 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
657 | 
658 |         // We do not want to warn about explicit comparisons to VOID. People
659 |         // often do this if they think their type annotations screwed up.
660 |         //
661 |         // We do want to warn about cases where people compare things like
662 |         // (Array|null) == (Function|null)
663 |         // because it probably means they screwed up.
664 |         //
665 |         // This heuristic here is not perfect, but should catch cases we
666 |         // care about without too many false negatives.
667 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
668 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
669 | 
670 |         TernaryValue result = TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
671 |         if (n.getType() == Token.EQ || n.getType() == Token.NE) {
672 |           result = leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
673 |           if (n.isNE()) {
674 |             result = result.not();
675 |           }
676 |         } else {
677 |           // SHEQ or SHNE
678 |           if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
679 |                   rightTypeRestricted)) {
680 |             result = n.getType() == Token.SHEQ ?
681 |                 TernaryValue.FALSE : TernaryValue.TRUE;
682 |           }
683 |         }
684 | 
685 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
686 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
687 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
688 |         }
689 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
690 |         break;
691 |       }
692 | 
693 |       case Token.LT:
694 |       case Token.LE:
695 |       case Token.GT:
696 |       case Token.GE:
697 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
698 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
699 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
700 |           validator.expectNumber(
701 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
702 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
703 |           validator.expectNumber(
704 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
705 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
706 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
707 |           // OK.
708 |         } else {
709 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
710 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
711 |           // should match a string context.
712 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
713 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
714 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
715 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
716 |           message = "right side of comparison";
717 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
718 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
719 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
720 |         }
721 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
722 |         break;
723 | 
724 |       case Token.IN:
725 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
726 |         right = n.getLastChild();
727 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
728 |         validator.expectString(t, left, getJSType(left), "left side of 'in'");
729 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
730 |         if (rightType.isStruct()) {
731 |           report(t, right, IN_USED_WITH_STRUCT);
732 |         }
733 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
734 |         break;
735 | 
736 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
737 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
738 |         right = n.getLastChild();
739 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
740 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
741 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "deterministic instanceof yields false");
742 |         validator.expectActualObject(
743 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
744 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
745 |         break;
746 | 
747 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
748 |         visitAssign(t, n);
749 |         typeable = false;
750 |         break;
751 | 
752 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
753 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
754 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
755 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
756 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
757 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
758 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
759 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
760 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
761 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
762 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
763 |         checkPropCreation(t, n.getFirstChild());
764 |         // fall through
765 | 
766 |       case Token.LSH:
767 |       case Token.RSH:
768 |       case Token.URSH:
769 |       case Token.DIV:
770 |       case Token.MOD:
771 |       case Token.BITOR:
772 |       case Token.BITXOR:
773 |       case Token.BITAND:
774 |       case Token.SUB:
775 |       case Token.ADD:
776 |       case Token.MUL:
777 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
778 |         break;
779 | 
780 |       case Token.DELPROP:
781 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
782 |         break;
783 | 
784 |       case Token.CASE:
785 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
786 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
787 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
788 |         typeable = false;
789 |         break;
790 | 
791 |       case Token.WITH: {
792 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
793 |         childType = getJSType(child);
794 |         validator.expectObject(t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
795 |         typeable = false;
796 |         break;
797 |       }
798 | 
799 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
800 |         visitFunction(t, n);
801 |         break;
802 | 
803 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
804 |       case Token.LABEL:
805 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
806 |       case Token.SWITCH:
807 |       case Token.BREAK:
808 |       case Token.CATCH:
809 |       case Token.TRY:
810 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
811 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
812 |       case Token.BLOCK:
813 |       case Token.EMPTY:
814 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
815 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
816 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
817 |       case Token.THROW:
818 |         typeable = false;
819 |         break;
820 | 
821 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
822 |       case Token.DO:
823 |       case Token.IF:
824 |       case Token.WHILE:
825 |         typeable = false;
826 |         break;
827 | 
828 |       case Token.FOR:
829 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
830 |           Node obj = n.getChildAtIndex(1);
831 |           if (getJSType(obj).isStruct()) {
832 |             report(t, obj, IN_USED_WITH_STRUCT);
833 |           }
834 |         }
835 |         typeable = false;
836 |         break;
837 | 
838 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
839 |       case Token.AND:
840 |       case Token.HOOK:
841 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
842 |       case Token.OR:
843 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
844 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
845 |         } else {

847 |           if ((n.isObjectLit())
848 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
849 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
850 |           } else {

852 |           }

855 |           JSType typ = getJSType(n);
856 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {

858 |           }
859 |         }
860 |         break;
861 | 
862 |       default:
863 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
864 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
865 |         break;
866 |     }
867 | 
868 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
869 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
870 | 
871 |     if (typeable) {
872 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
873 |     }
874 | 
875 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
876 |   }

===== M0967 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 399-425
Documentation:

Method code:
399 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
400 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
401 |     // two cases:
402 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
403 |     // and
404 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
405 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
406 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
407 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
408 |     //
409 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
410 |     //
411 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
412 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
413 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
414 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
415 |     //
416 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
417 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
418 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
419 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
420 |       typeOfThis.extendTemplateTypeMap(baseType.getTemplateTypeMap());
421 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
422 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
423 |     }
424 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
425 |   }

Original range: 1925-1929

/**
   * Is this node a hoisted function declaration? A function declaration in the

   * See {@link #isFunctionDeclaration}).

298 |   public boolean isDict() {

===== M0986 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1237-1297
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1237 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1238 |     // unknown
1239 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1240 |       return true;
1241 |     }
1242 |     // all type
1243 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1244 |       return true;
1245 |     }
1246 |     // equality
1247 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1248 |       return true;
1249 |     }
1250 |     // unions
1251 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1252 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1253 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1254 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1255 |           return true;
1256 |         }
1257 |       }
1258 |       return false;
1259 |     }
1260 | 
1261 |     // TemplateTypeMaps. This check only returns false if the TemplateTypeMaps
1262 |     // are not equivalent.
1263 |     TemplateTypeMap thisTypeParams = thisType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1264 |     TemplateTypeMap thatTypeParams = thatType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1265 |     boolean templateMatch = true;
1266 |     if (isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType)) {
1267 |       // Array and Object are exempt from template type invariance; their
1268 |       // template types maps are considered a match only if the ObjectElementKey
1269 |       // values are subtypes/supertypes of one another.
1270 |       TemplateType key = thisType.registry.getObjectElementKey();
1271 |       JSType thisElement = thisTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1272 |       JSType thatElement = thatTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1273 | 
1274 |       templateMatch = thisElement.isSubtype(thatElement)
1275 |           || thatElement.isSubtype(thisElement);
1276 |     } else {
1277 |       templateMatch = thisTypeParams.checkEquivalenceHelper(
1278 |           thatTypeParams, EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT);
1279 |     }
1280 |     if (!templateMatch) {
1281 |       return false;
1282 |     }
1283 | 
1284 |     // Templatized types. The above check guarantees TemplateTypeMap
1285 |     // equivalence; check if the base type is a subtype.
1286 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1287 |       return thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1288 |               thatType);
1289 |     }
1290 | 
1291 |     // proxy types
1292 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1293 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1294 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1295 |     }
1296 |     return false;
1297 |   }

===== M0988 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

Original range: 119-151
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns the type of a node in the given scope if the node corresponds to a
   * name whose type is capable of being refined.
   * @return The current type of the node if it can be refined, null otherwise.
   */
Method code:
119 |   protected JSType getTypeIfRefinable(Node node, FlowScope scope) {
120 |     switch (node.getType()) {
121 |       case Token.NAME:
122 |         StaticSlot<JSType> nameVar = scope.getSlot(node.getString());
123 |         if (nameVar != null) {
124 |           JSType nameVarType = nameVar.getType();
125 |           if (nameVarType == null) {
126 |             nameVarType = node.getJSType();
127 |           }
128 |           return nameVarType;
129 |         }
130 |         return null;
131 | 
132 |       case Token.GETPROP:
133 |         String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
134 |         if (qualifiedName == null) {
135 |           return null;
136 |         }
137 |         StaticSlot<JSType> propVar = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
138 |         JSType propVarType = null;
139 |         if (propVar != null) {
140 |           propVarType = propVar.getType();
141 |         }
142 |         if (propVarType == null) {
143 |           propVarType = node.getJSType();
144 |         }
145 |         if (propVarType == null) {
146 |           propVarType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
147 |         }
148 |         return propVarType;
149 |     }
150 |     return null;
151 |   }

89 |     super(cfg, new LinkedFlowScope.FlowScopeJoinOp());
90 |     this.compiler = compiler;
91 |     this.registry = compiler.getTypeRegistry();
92 |     this.reverseInterpreter = reverseInterpreter;
93 |     this.unknownType = registry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
94 | 
95 |     this.syntacticScope = functionScope;
96 |     inferArguments(functionScope);
97 | 
98 |     this.functionScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(functionScope);
99 |     this.assertionFunctionsMap = assertionFunctionsMap;
100 | 
101 |     // For each local variable declared with the VAR keyword, the entry
102 |     // type is VOID.
103 |     Iterator<Var> varIt =
104 |         functionScope.getDeclarativelyUnboundVarsWithoutTypes();
105 |     while (varIt.hasNext()) {
106 |       Var var = varIt.next();
107 |       if (isUnflowable(var)) {
108 |         continue;
109 |       }
110 | 
111 |       this.functionScope.inferSlotType(
112 |           var.getName(), getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
113 |     }
114 | 
115 |     this.bottomScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(
116 |         Scope.createLatticeBottom(functionScope.getRootNode()));
117 |   }

===== M1006 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.supAndInfHelper(FunctionType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 725-795
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the supremum or infimum of two functions.
   * Because sup() and inf() share a lot of logic for functions, we use
   * a single helper.
   * @param leastSuper If true, compute the supremum of {@code this} with
   *     {@code that}. Otherwise, compute the infimum.
   * @return The least supertype or greatest subtype.
   */
Method code:
725 |   FunctionType supAndInfHelper(FunctionType that, boolean leastSuper) {
726 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): When we remove the unknown type, the function types
727 |     // form a lattice with the universal constructor at the top of the lattice,
728 |     // and the LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE type at the bottom of the lattice.
729 |     //
730 |     // When we introduce the unknown type, it's much more difficult to make
731 |     // heads or tails of the partial ordering of types, because there's no
732 |     // clear hierarchy between the different components (parameter types and
733 |     // return types) in the ArrowType.
734 |     //
735 |     // Rather than make the situation more complicated by introducing new
736 |     // types (like unions of functions), we just fallback on the simpler
737 |     // approach of getting things right at the top and the bottom of the
738 |     // lattice.
739 |     //
740 |     // If there are unknown parameters or return types making things
741 |     // ambiguous, then sup(A, B) is always the top function type, and
742 |     // inf(A, B) is always the bottom function type.
743 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(that);
744 | 
745 |     if (isEquivalentTo(that)) {
746 |       return this;
747 |     }
748 | 
749 |     // If these are ordinary functions, then merge them.
750 |     // Don't do this if any of the params/return
751 |     // values are unknown, because then there will be cycles in
752 |     // their local lattice and they will merge in weird ways.
753 |     if (isOrdinaryFunction() && that.isOrdinaryFunction() &&
754 |         !this.call.hasUnknownParamsOrReturn() &&
755 |         !that.call.hasUnknownParamsOrReturn()) {
756 | 
757 |       // Check for the degenerate case, but double check
758 |       // that there's not a cycle.
759 |       boolean isSubtypeOfThat = isSubtype(that);
760 |       boolean isSubtypeOfThis = that.isSubtype(this);
761 |       if (isSubtypeOfThat && !isSubtypeOfThis) {
762 |         return leastSuper ? that : this;
763 |       } else if (isSubtypeOfThis && !isSubtypeOfThat) {
764 |         return leastSuper ? this : that;
765 |       }
766 | 
767 |       // Merge the two functions component-wise.
768 |       FunctionType merged = tryMergeFunctionPiecewise(that, leastSuper);
769 |       if (merged != null) {
770 |         return merged;
771 |       }
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     // The function instance type is a special case
775 |     // that lives above the rest of the lattice.
776 |     JSType functionInstance = registry.getNativeType(
777 |         JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE);
778 |     if (functionInstance.isEquivalentTo(that)) {
779 |       return leastSuper ? that : this;
780 |     } else if (functionInstance.isEquivalentTo(this)) {
781 |       return leastSuper ? this : that;
782 |     }
783 | 
784 |     // In theory, we should be using the GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE as the
785 |     // greatest function. In practice, we don't because it's way too
786 |     // broad. The greatest function takes var_args None parameters, which
787 |     // means that all parameters register a type warning.
788 |     //
789 |     // Instead, we use the U2U ctor type, which has unknown type args.
790 |     FunctionType greatestFn =
791 |         registry.getNativeFunctionType(JSTypeNative.U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
792 |     FunctionType leastFn =
793 |         registry.getNativeFunctionType(JSTypeNative.LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
794 |     return leastSuper ? greatestFn : leastFn;
795 |   }

===== M1013 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 538-551
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
538 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
539 |       Node ret;
540 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
541 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name) n);
542 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
543 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral) n);
544 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
545 |       } else {
546 |         ret = transform(n);
547 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
548 |       }
549 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
550 |       return ret;
551 |     }

===== M1019 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.getPropertyType(JSType,String,Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1302-1363
Documentation:

Method code:
1302 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1303 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1304 |     // We often have a couple of different types to choose from for the
1305 |     // property. Ordered by accuracy, we have
1306 |     // 1) A locally inferred qualified name (which is in the FlowScope)
1307 |     // 2) A globally declared qualified name (which is in the FlowScope)
1308 |     // 3) A property on the owner type (which is on objType)
1309 |     // 4) A name in the type registry (as a last resort)
1310 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1311 |     boolean isLocallyInferred = false;
1312 | 
1313 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1314 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1315 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1316 |     if (var != null) {
1317 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1318 |       if (varType != null) {
1319 |         boolean isDeclared = !var.isTypeInferred();
1320 |         isLocallyInferred = (var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName));
1321 |         if (isDeclared || isLocallyInferred) {
1322 |           propertyType = varType;
1323 |         }
1324 |       }
1325 |     }
1326 | 
1327 |     if (propertyType == null && objType != null) {
1328 |       JSType foundType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1329 |       if (foundType != null) {
1330 |         propertyType = foundType;
1331 |       }
1332 |     }
1333 | 
1334 |     if (propertyType != null && objType != null) {
1335 |       JSType restrictedObjType = objType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1336 |       if (restrictedObjType.isTemplatizedType()
1337 |           && propertyType.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
1338 |         TemplateTypeMap typeMap = restrictedObjType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1339 |         TemplateTypeMapReplacer replacer = new TemplateTypeMapReplacer(
1340 |             registry, typeMap);
1341 |         propertyType = propertyType.visit(replacer);
1342 |       }
1343 |     }
1344 | 
1345 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType())
1346 |         && qualifiedName != null) {
1347 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1348 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1349 |       if (regType != null) {
1350 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1351 |       }
1352 |     }
1353 | 
1354 |     if (propertyType == null) {
1355 |       return unknownType;
1356 |     } else if (propertyType.isEquivalentTo(unknownType) && isLocallyInferred) {
1357 |       // If the type has been checked in this scope,
1358 |       // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1359 |       return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1360 |     } else {
1361 |       return propertyType;
1362 |     }
1363 |   }

/**
   * Infer the template type from the doc info.
   */
Method code:
577 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferTemplateTypeName(
578 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info, JSType ownerType) {
579 |     if (info != null &&  !info.getTemplateTypeNames().isEmpty()) {
580 |       ImmutableList.Builder<TemplateType> builder = ImmutableList.builder();
581 |       for (String key : info.getTemplateTypeNames()) {
582 |         builder.add(typeRegistry.createTemplateType(key));
583 |       }
584 |       templateTypeNames = builder.build();
585 |     } else {
586 |       templateTypeNames = ImmutableList.of();
587 |     }
588 | 
589 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> keys = templateTypeNames;
590 |     if (ownerType != null) {
591 |       ImmutableList<TemplateType> ownerTypeKeys =
592 |           ownerType.getTemplateTypeMap().getTemplateKeys();
593 |       if (!ownerTypeKeys.isEmpty()) {
594 |         ImmutableList.Builder<TemplateType> builder = ImmutableList.builder();
595 |         builder.addAll(templateTypeNames);
596 |         builder.addAll(ownerTypeKeys);
597 |         keys = builder.build();
598 |       }
599 |     }
600 | 
601 |     if (!keys.isEmpty()) {
602 |       typeRegistry.setTemplateTypeNames(keys);
603 |     }
604 |     return this;
605 |   }

===== M1055 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,CompilerInput,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 520-580
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   * @return The variable we end up with. Most of the time, this will just
   *     be {@code var}, but in some rare cases we will need to declare
   *     a new var with new source info.
   */
Method code:
520 |   Var expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, CompilerInput input,
521 |       Node n, Node parent, Var var, String variableName, JSType newType) {
522 |     Var newVar = var;
523 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
524 |     if (n.isGetProp() ||
525 |         NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n)) {
526 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
527 |       if (info == null) {
528 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
529 |       }
530 |       allowDupe =
531 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
535 | 
536 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
537 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
538 |     // compilation process.
539 |     if (varType != null &&
540 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
541 |         newType != null &&
542 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
543 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
544 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
545 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
546 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
547 |       // native type. We should redeclare it at the new input site.
548 |       if (var.input == null) {
549 |         Scope s = var.getScope();
550 |         s.undeclare(var);
551 |         newVar = s.declare(variableName, n, varType, input, false);
552 | 
553 |         n.setJSType(varType);
554 |         if (parent.isVar()) {
555 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
556 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
557 |           }
558 |         } else {
559 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.isFunction());
560 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
561 |         }
562 |       } else {
563 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
564 |         // match the original type.
565 |         //
566 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
567 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
568 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
569 |               var.getParentNode().isExprResult()) ||
570 |             !newType.isEquivalentTo(varType)) {
571 |           report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
572 |               variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
573 |               String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
574 |               varType.toString()));
575 |         }
576 |       }
577 |     }
578 | 
579 |     return newVar;
580 |   }

Method code:
357 |   private void handleIf(Node node) {
358 |     Node thenBlock = node.getFirstChild().getNext();
359 |     Node elseBlock = thenBlock.getNext();
360 |     createEdge(node, Branch.ON_TRUE, computeFallThrough(thenBlock));
361 | 
362 |     if (elseBlock == null) {
363 |       createEdge(node, Branch.ON_FALSE,
364 |           computeFollowNode(node, this)); // not taken branch

367 |     }

369 |         node, NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(node));

===== M1074 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getRemainingJSDocLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 199-223
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the remaining JSDoc line without the {@link JsDocToken#EOL},
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOF} or {@link JsDocToken#EOC}.
   */
Method code:
199 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
200 |   String getRemainingJSDocLine() {
201 |     int c;
202 |     for (;;) {
203 |       c = getChar();
204 |       switch (c) {
205 |         case '*':
206 |           if (peekChar() != '/') {
207 |             addToString(c);
208 |             break;
209 |           }
210 |           // fall through
211 |         case EOF_CHAR:
212 |         case '\n':
213 |           ungetChar(c);
214 |           this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
215 |           stringBufferTop = 0;
216 |           return this.string;
217 | 
218 |         default:
219 |           addToString(c);
220 |           break;
221 |       }
222 |     }
223 |   }

/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.
   *
   * For the purposes of this function, we define a MaybeFunctionType as any
   * type in the sub-lattice
   * { x | LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE <= x <= GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE }
   * This definition excludes bottom types like NoType and NoObjectType.
   *
   * This definition is somewhat arbitrary and axiomatic, but this is the
   * definition that makes the most sense for the most callers.
   */
Method code:
350 |   public FunctionType toMaybeFunctionType() {
351 |     return null;
352 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)

Original range: 529-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
529 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
530 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
531 |          current != null;
532 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
533 |       if (current.isTemplatizedType()) {
534 |         current = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType();
535 |       }
536 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
537 |         return true;
538 |       }
539 |     }
540 |     return false;
541 |   }

===== M1083 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 756-838
Documentation:

Method code:
756 |     @Override
757 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
758 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
759 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
760 |       if (name == null) {
761 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
762 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
763 |           errorReporter.error(
764 |             "unnamed function statement",
765 |             sourceName,
766 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
767 | 
768 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
769 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
770 |         }
771 |         name = new Name();
772 |         name.setIdentifier("");
773 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
774 |       }
775 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
776 |       Node newName = transform(name);
777 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
778 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
779 |         // declaration.
780 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
781 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
782 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
783 |         // same line as the function keyword.
784 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
785 |             functionNode.getLp();
786 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
787 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
788 |       }
789 | 
790 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
791 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
792 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
793 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
794 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
795 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
796 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
797 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
798 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
799 |       if (fnName != null) {
800 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
801 |       } else {
802 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
803 |       }
804 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
805 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
806 | 
807 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
808 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
809 |         Node paramNode = transformParameter(param);
810 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
811 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
812 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
813 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
814 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
815 |         } else {
816 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
817 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
818 |           // (an error has already been reported).
819 |           Preconditions.checkState(
820 |               config.isIdeMode
821 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
822 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
823 |         }
824 |       }
825 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
826 | 
827 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
828 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
829 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
830 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
831 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
832 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
833 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
834 |       }
835 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
836 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
837 |      return node;
838 |     }

===== M1086 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 79-171
Documentation:

Method code:
79 |   @Override
80 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType other) {
81 |     if (!(other instanceof ArrowType)) {
82 |       return false;
83 |     }
84 | 
85 |     ArrowType that = (ArrowType) other;
86 | 
87 |     // This is described in Draft 2 of the ES4 spec,
88 |     // Section 3.4.7: Subtyping Function Types.
89 | 
90 |     // this.returnType <: that.returnType (covariant)
91 |     if (!this.returnType.isSubtype(that.returnType)) {
92 |       return false;
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     // that.paramType[i] <: this.paramType[i] (contravariant)
96 |     //
97 |     // If this.paramType[i] is required,
98 |     // then that.paramType[i] is required.
99 |     //
100 |     // In theory, the "required-ness" should work in the other direction as
101 |     // well. In other words, if we have
102 |     //
103 |     // function f(number, number) {}
104 |     // function g(number) {}
105 |     //
106 |     // Then f *should* not be a subtype of g, and g *should* not be
107 |     // a subtype of f. But in practice, we do not implement it this way.
108 |     // We want to support the use case where you can pass g where f is
109 |     // expected, and pretend that g ignores the second argument.
110 |     // That way, you can have a single "no-op" function, and you don't have
111 |     // to create a new no-op function for every possible type signature.
112 |     //
113 |     // So, in this case, g < f, but f !< g
114 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
115 |     Node thatParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
116 |     while (thisParam != null && thatParam != null) {
117 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
118 |       JSType thatParamType = thatParam.getJSType();
119 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
120 |         if (thatParamType == null ||
121 |             !thatParamType.isSubtype(thisParamType)) {
122 |           return false;
123 |         }
124 |       }
125 | 
126 |       boolean thisIsVarArgs = thisParam.isVarArgs();
127 |       boolean thatIsVarArgs = thatParam.isVarArgs();
128 |       boolean thisIsOptional = thisIsVarArgs || thisParam.isOptionalArg();
129 |       boolean thatIsOptional = thatIsVarArgs || thatParam.isOptionalArg();
130 | 
131 |       // "that" can't be a supertype, because it's missing a required argument.
132 |       if (!thisIsOptional && thatIsOptional) {
133 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): In our type system, we use {function(...?)} and
134 |         // {function(...NoType)} to to indicate that arity should not be
135 |         // checked. Strictly speaking, this is not a correct formulation,
136 |         // because now a sub-function can required arguments that are var_args
137 |         // in the super-function. So we special-case this.
138 |         boolean isTopFunction =
139 |             thatIsVarArgs &&
140 |             (thatParamType == null ||
141 |              thatParamType.isUnknownType() ||
142 |              thatParamType.isNoType());
143 |         if (!isTopFunction) {
144 |           return false;
145 |         }
146 |       }
147 | 
148 |       // don't advance if we have variable arguments
149 |       if (!thisIsVarArgs) {
150 |         thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
151 |       }
152 |       if (!thatIsVarArgs) {
153 |         thatParam = thatParam.getNext();
154 |       }
155 | 
156 |       // both var_args indicates the end
157 |       if (thisIsVarArgs && thatIsVarArgs) {
158 |         thisParam = null;
159 |         thatParam = null;
160 |       }
161 |     }
162 | 
163 |     // "that" can't be a supertype, because it's missing a required argument.
164 |     if (thisParam != null
165 |         && !thisParam.isOptionalArg() && !thisParam.isVarArgs()
166 |         && thatParam == null) {
167 |       return false;
168 |     }
169 | 
170 |     return true;
171 |   }

===== M1099 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkPropCreation(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1030-1062
Documentation:
/**
   * After a struct object is created, we can't add new properties to it, with
   * one exception. We allow creation of "static" properties like
   * Foo.prototype.bar = baz;
   * where Foo.prototype is a struct, if the assignment happens at the top level
   * and the constructor Foo is defined in the same file.
   */
Method code:
1030 |   private void checkPropCreation(NodeTraversal t, Node lvalue) {
1031 |     if (lvalue.isGetProp()) {
1032 |       Node obj = lvalue.getFirstChild();
1033 |       Node prop = lvalue.getLastChild();
1034 |       JSType objType = getJSType(obj);
1035 |       String pname = prop.getString();
1036 | 
1037 |       if (!objType.isStruct() || objType.hasProperty(pname)) {
1038 |         return;
1039 |       }
1040 |       Scope s = t.getScope();
1041 |       if (obj.isThis() && getJSType(s.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
1042 |         return;
1043 |       }
1044 |       // Prop created outside ctor, check that it's a static prop
1045 |       Node assgnExp = lvalue.getParent();
1046 |       Node assgnStm = assgnExp.getParent();
1047 |       if (objType instanceof ObjectType &&
1048 |           s.isGlobal() &&
1049 |           NodeUtil.isPrototypePropertyDeclaration(assgnStm)) {
1050 |         ObjectType instance =
1051 |             objType.toObjectType().getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType();
1052 |         String file = lvalue.getSourceFileName();
1053 |         Node ctor = instance.getConstructor().getSource();
1054 |         if (ctor != null && ctor.getSourceFileName().equals(file)) {
1055 |           JSType rvalueType = assgnExp.getLastChild().getJSType();
1056 |           instance.defineInferredProperty(pname, rvalueType, lvalue);
1057 |           return;
1058 |         }
1059 |       }
1060 |       report(t, prop, ILLEGAL_PROPERTY_CREATION);
1061 |     }
1062 |   }

203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.type.SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.caseEquality(Node,Node,FlowScope,Function<TypePair, TypePair>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/type/SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 271-304
Documentation:

Method code:
271 |   private FlowScope caseEquality(Node left, Node right, FlowScope blindScope,
272 |       Function<TypePair, TypePair> merging) {
273 |     // left type
274 |     JSType leftType = getTypeIfRefinable(left, blindScope);
275 |     boolean leftIsRefineable;
276 |     if (leftType != null) {
277 |       leftIsRefineable = true;
278 |     } else {
279 |       leftIsRefineable = false;
280 |       leftType = left.getJSType();
281 |     }
282 | 
283 |     // right type
284 |     JSType rightType = getTypeIfRefinable(right, blindScope);
285 |     boolean rightIsRefineable;
286 |     if (rightType != null) {
287 |       rightIsRefineable = true;
288 |     } else {
289 |       rightIsRefineable = false;
290 |       rightType = right.getJSType();
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     // merged types
294 |     TypePair merged = merging.apply(new TypePair(leftType, rightType));
295 | 
296 |     // creating new scope
297 |     if (merged != null) {
298 |       return maybeRestrictTwoNames(
299 |           blindScope,
300 |           left, leftType, leftIsRefineable ? merged.typeA : null,
301 |           right, rightType, rightIsRefineable ? merged.typeB : null);
302 |     }
303 |     return blindScope;
304 |   }

===== M1112 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(JsDocToken,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2122-2161
Documentation:
/**
   * Create a new union type, with an alternate that has already been
   * parsed. The alternate may be null.
   */
Method code:
2122 |   private Node parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(JsDocToken token, Node alternate) {
2123 |     Node union = newNode(Token.PIPE);
2124 |     if (alternate != null) {
2125 |       union.addChildToBack(alternate);
2126 |     }
2127 | 
2128 |     Node expr = null;
2129 |     do {
2130 |       if (expr != null) {
2131 |         skipEOLs();
2132 |         token = next();
2133 |         Preconditions.checkState(
2134 |             token == JsDocToken.PIPE || token == JsDocToken.COMMA);
2135 | 
2136 |         boolean isPipe = token == JsDocToken.PIPE;
2137 |         if (isPipe && match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
2138 |           // We support double pipes for backwards compatibility.
2139 |           next();
2140 |         }
2141 |         skipEOLs();
2142 |         token = next();
2143 |       }
2144 |       expr = parseTypeExpression(token);
2145 |       if (expr == null) {
2146 |         return null;
2147 |       }
2148 | 
2149 |       union.addChildToBack(expr);
2150 |       // We support commas for backwards compatibility.
2151 |     } while (match(JsDocToken.PIPE, JsDocToken.COMMA));
2152 | 
2153 |     if (alternate == null) {
2154 |       skipEOLs();
2155 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RP)) {
2156 |         return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rp");
2157 |       }
2158 |       next();
2159 |     }
2160 |     return union;
2161 |   }

===== M1126 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1573-1588
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1573 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1574 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1575 |       String name = getString();
1576 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1577 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1578 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1579 |       if (left == null) {
1580 |         return null;
1581 |       }
1582 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1583 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1584 |       return "this";
1585 |     } else {
1586 |       return null;
1587 |     }
1588 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitVar(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1622-1651
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1622 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1623 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1624 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1625 |     // merge to fix this.
1626 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1627 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1628 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1629 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1630 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1631 | 
1632 |       if (value != null) {
1633 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1634 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1635 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1636 | 
1637 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1638 |         if (info == null) {
1639 |           info = varInfo;
1640 |         }
1641 | 
1642 |         checkEnumAlias(t, info, value);
1643 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1644 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1645 |         } else {
1646 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1647 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1648 |         }
1649 |       }
1650 |     }
1651 |   }

===== M1137 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.mayThrowException(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 910-931
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
910 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
911 |     switch (n.getType()) {
912 |       case Token.CALL:
913 |       case Token.GETPROP:
914 |       case Token.GETELEM:
915 |       case Token.THROW:
916 |       case Token.NEW:
917 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
918 |       case Token.INC:
919 |       case Token.DEC:
920 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
921 |         return true;
922 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
923 |         return false;
924 |     }
925 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
926 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
927 |         return true;
928 |       }
929 |     }
930 |     return false;
931 |   }
