===== M1144 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.getPropertyType(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 593-625
Documentation:

Method code:
593 |   @Override
594 |   public JSType getPropertyType(String name) {
595 |     if (!hasOwnProperty(name)) {
596 |       // Define the "call", "apply", and "bind" functions lazily.
597 |       boolean isCall = "call".equals(name);
598 |       boolean isBind = "bind".equals(name);
599 |       if (isCall || isBind) {
600 |         defineDeclaredProperty(name, getCallOrBindSignature(isCall), source);
601 |       } else if ("apply".equals(name)) {
602 |         // Define the "apply" function lazily.
603 |         FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(registry);
604 | 
605 |         // ECMA-262 says that apply's second argument must be an Array
606 |         // or an arguments object. We don't model the arguments object,
607 |         // so let's just be forgiving for now.
608 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Model the Arguments object.
609 |         builder.addOptionalParams(
610 |             registry.createNullableType(getTypeOfThis()),
611 |             registry.createNullableType(
612 |                 registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE)));
613 | 
614 |         defineDeclaredProperty(name,
615 |             new FunctionBuilder(registry)
616 |             .withParams(builder)
617 |             .withReturnType(getReturnType())
618 |             .withTemplateKeys(getTemplateTypeMap().getTemplateKeys())
619 |             .build(),
620 |             source);
621 |       }
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     return super.getPropertyType(name);
625 |   }

===== M1151 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType,String,JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1195-1320
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor type and a property name, check that the property has
   * the JSDoc annotation @override iff the property is declared on a
   * superclass. Several checks regarding inheritance correctness are also
   * performed.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   private void checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
1196 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, FunctionType ctorType, String propertyName,
1197 |       JSDocInfo info, JSType propertyType) {
1198 |     // If the supertype doesn't resolve correctly, we've warned about this
1199 |     // already.
1200 |     if (hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(ctorType)) {
1201 |       return;
1202 |     }
1203 | 
1204 |     FunctionType superClass = ctorType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1205 |     boolean superClassHasProperty = superClass != null &&
1206 |         superClass.getInstanceType().hasProperty(propertyName);
1207 |     boolean superClassHasDeclaredProperty = superClass != null &&
1208 |         superClass.getInstanceType().isPropertyTypeDeclared(propertyName);
1209 | 
1210 |     // For interface
1211 |     boolean superInterfaceHasProperty = false;
1212 |     boolean superInterfaceHasDeclaredProperty = false;
1213 |     if (ctorType.isInterface()) {
1214 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1215 |         superInterfaceHasProperty =
1216 |             superInterfaceHasProperty ||
1217 |             interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName);
1218 |         superInterfaceHasDeclaredProperty =
1219 |             superInterfaceHasDeclaredProperty ||
1220 |             interfaceType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propertyName);
1221 |       }
1222 |     }
1223 |     boolean declaredOverride = info != null && info.isOverride();
1224 | 
1225 |     boolean foundInterfaceProperty = false;
1226 |     if (ctorType.isConstructor()) {
1227 |       for (JSType implementedInterface :
1228 |           ctorType.getAllImplementedInterfaces()) {
1229 |         if (implementedInterface.isUnknownType() ||
1230 |             implementedInterface.isEmptyType()) {
1231 |           continue;
1232 |         }
1233 |         FunctionType interfaceType =
1234 |             implementedInterface.toObjectType().getConstructor();
1235 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(interfaceType);
1236 | 
1237 |         boolean interfaceHasProperty =
1238 |             interfaceType.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1239 |         foundInterfaceProperty = foundInterfaceProperty ||
1240 |             interfaceHasProperty;
1241 |         if (reportMissingOverride.isOn()
1242 |             && !declaredOverride
1243 |             && interfaceHasProperty) {
1244 |           // @override not present, but the property does override an interface
1245 |           // property
1246 |           compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1247 |               HIDDEN_INTERFACE_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1248 |               interfaceType.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName).toString()));
1249 |         }
1250 |       }
1251 |     }
1252 | 
1253 |     if (!declaredOverride
1254 |         && !superClassHasProperty
1255 |         && !superInterfaceHasProperty) {
1256 |       // nothing to do here, it's just a plain new property
1257 |       return;
1258 |     }
1259 | 
1260 |     ObjectType topInstanceType = superClassHasDeclaredProperty ?
1261 |         superClass.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName) : null;
1262 |     boolean declaredLocally =
1263 |         ctorType.isConstructor() &&
1264 |         (ctorType.getPrototype().hasOwnProperty(propertyName) ||
1265 |          ctorType.getInstanceType().hasOwnProperty(propertyName));
1266 |     if (reportMissingOverride.isOn()
1267 |         && !declaredOverride
1268 |         && superClassHasDeclaredProperty
1269 |         && declaredLocally) {
1270 |       // @override not present, but the property does override a superclass
1271 |       // property
1272 |       compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1273 |           HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1274 |           topInstanceType.toString()));
1275 |     }
1276 | 
1277 |     // @override is present and we have to check that it is ok
1278 |     if (superClassHasDeclaredProperty) {
1279 |       // there is a superclass implementation
1280 |       JSType superClassPropType =
1281 |           superClass.getInstanceType().getPropertyType(propertyName);
1282 |       TemplateTypeMap ctorTypeMap =
1283 |           ctorType.getTypeOfThis().getTemplateTypeMap();
1284 |       if (!ctorTypeMap.isEmpty()) {
1285 |         superClassPropType = superClassPropType.visit(
1286 |             new TemplateTypeMapReplacer(typeRegistry, ctorTypeMap));
1287 |       }
1288 | 
1289 |       if (!propertyType.isSubtype(superClassPropType)) {
1290 |         compiler.report(
1291 |             t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1292 |                 propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1293 |                 superClassPropType.toString(), propertyType.toString()));
1294 |       }
1295 |     } else if (superInterfaceHasDeclaredProperty) {
1296 |       // there is an super interface property
1297 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1298 |         if (interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName)) {
1299 |           JSType superPropertyType =
1300 |               interfaceType.getPropertyType(propertyName);
1301 |           if (!propertyType.isSubtype(superPropertyType)) {
1302 |             topInstanceType = interfaceType.getConstructor().
1303 |                 getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName);
1304 |             compiler.report(
1305 |                 t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1306 |                     propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1307 |                     superPropertyType.toString(),
1308 |                     propertyType.toString()));
1309 |           }
1310 |         }
1311 |       }
1312 |     } else if (!foundInterfaceProperty
1313 |         && !superClassHasProperty
1314 |         && !superInterfaceHasProperty) {
1315 |       // there is no superclass nor interface implementation
1316 |       compiler.report(
1317 |           t.makeError(n, UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE,
1318 |               propertyName, ctorType.getInstanceType().toString()));
1319 |     }
1320 |   }
